Contract: `CC-12`, `CC-17`; WT-03, STK-05, STK-07, SET-04.
- **review-05 · Consent in the terminal: prompts, flags and agents.** Options: (a) under `AGENT=1` `caddy trust` refuses before `sudo` and tells the agent to ask the person [first-run-setup]; (b) `trust-remote` shows the fingerprint and asks, with `--yes` and an `untrust` [first-run-setup]; (c) multi-stack commands ask in a human terminal unless `--yes` [stack-run-stop]; (d) `caddy stop` refuses without a terminal unless `--force` [first-run-setup, stack-run-stop]; (e) say first that a step downloads or asks for a password [proxy-settings]; (f) chat agents run with `AGENT=1` [agent-chat]. Decision (revision 2): (a), (b), (f), and (c) only when the set would end an agent's turn (`O33`), `REV-7`, `REV-8`, `UNDO-5`; (d) gives way to (c); (e) is 2.12's help wording. Reason: a cached `sudo` ticket would let an agent install a root silently, so trust alone needs the person; one skip flag avoids two spellings. Must change: first-run-setup, stack-run-stop, proxy-settings, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: serve-folder (`devhost serve`'s trust step follows `REV-8`). Contract: `CC-12`, `CC-13`; SET-03, SET-05, SET-06, STK-09.
- **review-06 · Review before a config editor save.** Options: (a) name beside Save every service the save restarts, report per service, offer Undo save [config-editor, manifest-hot-reload]; (b) turning the editor off over other drafts asks [config-editor]; (c) after a rejection, mark the lines that differ from what runs, with Restore Running Version [config-editor]; (d) a devhost config file save reports its changed keys [proxy-settings]; (e) every reload accounts for itself [manifest-hot-reload]. Decision: all but Undo save, `REV-10`, `REV-3`, `UNDO-6`. Reason: settings apply through hot reload (owner rule), so the reach sits beside Save; Undo save would present a second reload as a reversal, while the editor's undo survives a save, as in VS Code. Must change: config-editor, manifest-hot-reload, proxy-settings. Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery (Edit from the crash view opens a window whose Save shows the same reach). Contract: none; CFG-04, CFG-05, MAN-03, SET-08.
- **review-07 · What a hot reload may do without review.** Options: (a) no unchanged failing service as a condition, and per-service rollback [manifest-hot-reload, services-panel]; (b) probe new fixed ports first [manifest-hot-reload]; (c) restart only what the edit needs [manifest-hot-reload]; (d) a newer save supersedes [manifest-hot-reload]; (e) say when switching turns off [manifest-hot-reload, worktree-switch]; (f) chat off keeps conversations reachable [agent-chat]; (g) never remove Hold Reloads as a side effect [toolbar-shell]. Decision: all, `REV-10`, `REV-11`, `REV-12`, `UNDO-1`, `HOLD-4`, `HOLD-5`, `SEL-11`; (c)'s default is settled by `O9` (decided 2026-11-18). Reason: no position asks for a review step, as the owner rule requires; Docker Compose and Kubernetes keep healthy services up when another fails. Must change: manifest-hot-reload, services-panel, worktree-switch, agent-chat, toolbar-shell. Silent, must adopt: config-editor (its saves report through this reload), proxy-settings (a devhost config file change is checked before Caddy moves, `REV-11`). Contract: `CC-18`; MAN-03, MAN-04, CHAT-12.
- **review-08 · Disable Reloads: the request reviewed before it goes to an agent.** Options: (a) q5: keep the label; a popover on the hot reload notice names the agent and shows the editable request for this worktree, with Send, Cancel and Hold Reloads [reload-control]; (b) the same, renamed, with Cancel leaving nothing behind and an undoable Dismiss [reload-control]; (c) a pending state until the request takes effect [reload-control]. Decision: (a), with (b) but not its rename, `REV-5`, `UNDO-3`, `O2`, `O56`; (c) is 2.7's. Reason: the owner decided q5 (`O2`) and `O56` (Disable Reloads wins over auto-reload); the request runs in **This worktree (live)**. Must change: reload-control. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (the request shows in its conversation with its mode), notices-and-feedback (the anchor notice stays visible, `SURF-1`), toolbar-shell (its one-line form when collapsed, `SURF-8`). Contract: `CC-15`, `CC-20`; RLD-01.
- **review-09 · What chat shows before Send.** Options: (a) `O1`'s visible edit mode [agent-chat, page-context]; (b) a workflow line plus a one-time confirmation per project [agent-chat]; (c) an edit-mode control per conversation, never confirmed [agent-chat]; (d) agent, model and approval behavior named before the first Send [agent-chat]; (e) say when a Send starts a conversation or joins a turn [agent-chat]; (f) page context reviewed item by item [page-context]. Decision: (a), (c) to (f), `REV-6`, `SEL-8`, `SEL-9`, `O1`, `O3`, `O13`; (b)'s line becomes the mode's disclosure, without its confirmation. Reason: `O1` makes the handling visible state, not a question, as Claude Code, Cursor and Copilot do. Must change: agent-chat, page-context. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (its request shows its fixed mode), worktree-switch (a switch keeps harness, model and mode, `SEL-11`), running-agents (supplies the second-agent check). Contract: `CC-10`; CHAT-01, CHAT-03, CHAT-08, CTX-05.
- **review-10 · Answering an agent's approval or question.** Options: (a) the decision first and never scrolled away, Allow Once never on Enter [agent-chat]; (b) show the diff, or the command and its folder, under a plain title [agent-chat]; (c) Deny with a note [agent-chat]; (d) a permanent decision line, and a denied call never shown as done [agent-chat]. Decision (revision 5, product owner): all, plus the agent CLI's own standing approval on the decision card when the CLI offers one (`O12`, reversed 2026-11-18), with neither Allow Once nor the standing-approval option taking Enter; `REV-9`, `SURF-6`, `UNDO-8`. Reason: Claude Code, Codex CLI and Cursor show the change above the choice and let a refusal carry an instruction; the person, never devhost, grants a standing approval through the CLI's own option so later matching requests do not keep prompting during a long turn (superseding revision 4's recommendation of no standing approvals from the browser). Must change: agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: page-context (a picked element's fix asks through the same card), reload-control (its request's approvals too). Contract: `CC-16`; CHAT-06.
- **review-11 · Discarding pending work.** Options: (a) configuration drafts survive reloads, and turning the editor off over drafts asks [config-editor]; (b) a disk change never silently replaces an edit [config-editor]; (c) page context survives reloads, and a draft citing a lost mark asks [page-context]; (d) releasing the hold lets the held reload through, and a dismissed one stays marked [reload-control]; (e) an unsent message stays in the composer, never as an error [agent-chat]. Decision (revision 2): all, `UNDO-2`, `UNDO-3`, `UNDO-4`, `REV-3`, `REV-6`, with (d)'s released reload kept pending with **Reload Now** instead of reloading at once. Reason: chat already keeps its draft and says so, and every position extends that, as VS Code's hot exit does; a toggle that reloads the page at once changes context on input (WCAG 3.2.2) and discards a half-typed form. Must change: config-editor, page-context, reload-control, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch, services-panel and startup-and-recovery (their reloads keep work, `UNDO-2`, and name a held reload first, `REV-3`). Contract: `CC-19`, `CC-20`; CFG-05, CTX-03, RLD-02, CHAT-07.
- **review-12 · Chat controls that start, stop or end an agent's work.** Options: (a) a second agent asks, Cancel first, the consequence stated [agent-chat]; (b) Close Conversation keeps the transcript with Resume, behind an Undo or a question [agent-chat]; (c) changing the agent is an explicit new conversation [agent-chat]; (d) Stop acts at once, as the person's choice [agent-chat]. Decision (revision 5, product owner): (a) `REV-3`, only for a message in **This worktree (live)** (not **Make tickets**; `O1` removed the New worktree mode on 2026-11-18); (b) recoverable without a question, `UNDO-4`; (c) `SEL-8`; (d) `UNDO-4`, its outcome 2.7's. Reason: ask only where two agents would edit the same files, which a message in **Make tickets** never does; Claude Code and Codex interrupt without asking, and Cursor closes a chat and keeps it in history. Must change: agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: running-agents (supplies the agent the question names). Contract: `CC-14`; CHAT-04, CHAT-05, CHAT-07.
- **review-13 · A pending approval outside the chat panel.** Options: (a) a needs-you state on Chat and the running agents trigger [agent-chat]; (b) Watch Live Activity shows the wait [running-agents]; (c) another worktree's waiting conversation is answerable here [agent-chat]; (d) the switch names a waiting agent [worktree-switch]; (e) chat off leaves the request answerable [agent-chat]; (f) the collapsed badge covers everything pending [toolbar-shell]; (g) a Disable Reloads request keeps its notice slot [reload-control]. Decision: all, `SURF-8`, `SEL-8`, `REV-9` (one place to answer, one activation from every marker), `REV-3`, `HOLD-5`; (g) is 2.7's. Reason: a request devhost never answers must never wait unseen. Must change: agent-chat, running-agents, worktree-switch, toolbar-shell, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: notices-and-feedback (a waiting agent's notice follows 2.7). Contract: `CC-18`; CHAT-06, AGT-01, AGT-02.
- **review-14 · Acting on stale state.** Options: (a) while disconnected, the last known list with every write disabled and its reason [notices-and-feedback, services-panel]; (b) labels say what an action acts on now [services-panel]; (c) labels follow what runs, and no deleted worktree is preselected [worktree-switch]; (d) the editor follows the disk [config-editor]; (e) the stack directory updates in place [stack-directory]; (f) pages learn a new origin first [proxy-settings, notices-and-feedback]. Decision: all, `REV-13`, `HOLD-2`, `SEL-1`, `SEL-4`, `UNDO-3`; (e) and how (a) is drawn are 2.10's. Reason: the server already refuses stale writes; each position adds the page's side, as VS Code Remote does. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, worktree-switch, config-editor, stack-directory, proxy-settings. Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery (status page buttons follow `HOLD-2` and `REV-13`). Contract: none; TB-07, CFG-05, REC-07.
- **review-15 · Repeated, overlapping and unanswered requests.** Options: (a) chat never resends [agent-chat]; (b) queue a save and name it [config-editor]; (c) queue a restart and show it waiting [services-panel]; (d) refuse the restart key with a reason [services-panel]; (e) a newer manifest supersedes [manifest-hot-reload]; (f) every click shows an outcome [agent-chat]. Decision: `REV-12`, `HOLD-3`, `SEL-7`: queue for another target (b, c), refuse for the same target or a stack-wide clash (d), supersede (e), never replay (a, f). Reason: the cases differ; Terraform and Helm refuse an overlapping run with a reason, and IDEs queue a second build. Must change: config-editor, services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (unavailable with its reason during another operation), startup-and-recovery (a lost response is read from the published state), stack-run-stop (`devhost stop` cancels a start and says so). Contract: `CC-8`; SVC-02, SVC-03, CFG-04, MAN-03, CHAT-03.
- **review-16 · Who may trigger an agent or a disruptive action.** Options: (a) only loopback clients for requests that run code [agent-chat]; (b) serve sessions start with chat off [serve-folder]; (c) an honest network boundary [proxy-settings]; (d) owner-settled: configuration saves only from this machine, Watch Live Activity on 127.0.0.1 [config-editor, running-agents]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): under `HOLD-8`, this machine stays trusted and remote browsers authenticate with one shared WebAuthn passkey under a single owned parent HTTPS domain (phone support required via synced passkeys and hybrid/QR sign-in), with one-time `devhost pair` links kept for bootstrapping, non-domain hostnames and lockout recovery (`O36`, decided 2026-11-25, `HOLD-7`), while unauthenticated remote clients see the unauthenticated state; authenticated remote browsers read and save configuration (`O38`, `O54`, both decided 2026-11-18) and open Watch Live Activity on the stack directory's hostname (`O55`, decided 2026-11-18); `devhost serve` keeps chat on by default, stated on the first line, with `--no-chat` to turn it off (`O40`, reversed 2026-11-18); on macOS, `bindHost = "127.0.0.1"` listens on loopback from port 1024 upward, and below 1024 (including default 443) keeps listening on all interfaces while printing an explicit warning naming the fix (`O39`, decided 2026-11-25). Reason: revision 3 had decided (d) alone and left (a) to (c) outside this reference; revision 4 brought who may act into this reference under `HOLD-8`; revision 5 approved `O38`, `O54`, `O55` and reversed `O40`; on 2026-11-25 the owner reversed `O36` from pairing-only to one shared-domain passkey with phone support and pairing fallback, and decided `O39` to preserve existing port-443 setups with a warning while honoring loopback from port 1024 upward. Must change: config-editor, agent-chat, serve-folder, running-agents, proxy-settings. Silent, must adopt: services-panel, worktree-switch, startup-and-recovery, reload-control (each shows the unauthenticated state where its writes are reachable, `HOLD-7`). Contract: the Security model of `reference/control-api.md` (`O36`); CFG-04, AGT-02.
- **review-17 · The form of a confirmation.** Options: (a) in place, Enter confirms [services-panel]; (b) inline, the safe choice focused [worktree-switch]; (c) the consequence stated, Cancel the default, Allow never on Enter [agent-chat]; (d) a popover on the notice [reload-control]; (e) the terminal lists its targets, then `[y/N]` [first-run-setup, stack-run-stop]; (f) the question names what is lost [config-editor, page-context]. Decision: `REV-4` with (b) to (f), placed per `SURF-2`; (a)'s Enter is overruled. Reason: Apple's guidelines advise against a destructive default, and with Enter on the confirm a double press restarts the stack the question protects. Must change: services-panel. Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery (the same form on status pages), toolbar-shell (questions stay above page popovers, `SURF-11`), notices-and-feedback (no question in an outcome notice). Contract: none.

### 3.3 feedback

- **feedback-01 · Where progress of a page-started operation shows.** Options: (a) one shared activity slot at the dock edge [notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell]; (b) step-level progress in the surface that started it [services-panel, worktree-switch, config-editor, startup-and-recovery]; (c) a save from any editor names itself and its restarts [manifest-hot-reload]; (d) no working indicator before work starts [agent-chat]. Decision: (b) while that surface is open, (a) as an activity line otherwise, with (c) and (d); `FB-1`, `ACT-1`, `ACT-3`, `HOLD-3`. Reason: the positions differ in grain and home, not intent; one home at a time avoids repeated indicators, as VS Code shows a task where it started and in its status bar. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, worktree-switch, config-editor, startup-and-recovery, manifest-hot-reload, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (the Disable Reloads request is pending in its notice slot, `FB-5`), page-context (tab capture and cropping show progress in the composer), stack-directory (Refresh shows progress in place). Contract: none; TB-07, SVC-02, SVC-04, WT-02, CFG-04.
- **feedback-02 · Success outcome: whether, where and in what sentence.** Options: (a) every success in a shared slot, fading after about 4 s, kept in an activity list [notices-and-feedback]; (b) Restart Stack's success stays until dismissed [services-panel]; (c) every reload names its restarts or "nothing restarted" [manifest-hot-reload, config-editor]; (d) the crash view shows the recovery before it closes [startup-and-recovery]; (e) a switch's result survives its reload [worktree-switch]; (f) terminal lines state results, not paths [stack-run-stop, proxy-settings, first-run-setup, serve-folder]; (g) small actions confirm their effect [reload-control, component-source]; (h) the directory states its freshness [stack-directory]. Decision: all, with (a)'s lifetime for every success notice (`O14`, refined by `O53` on 2026-11-18), a success kept while its own surface is open, and the stack's log as the activity list; `FB-2`, `FB-3`, `FB-6`, `FB-7`, `FB-10`, `ACT-12`. Reason: VS Code and JetBrains let routine successes fade, and the log keeps each one. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, startup-and-recovery, worktree-switch, stack-run-stop, proxy-settings, first-run-setup, serve-folder, reload-control, component-source, stack-directory. Silent, must adopt: page-context (the sent message shows the page context it carried, `UNDO-8`). Contract: `CC-21`, `CC-22`; SVC-02, SVC-04, MAN-03, CFG-04.
- **feedback-03 · Where an outcome lives once its surface closes.** Options: (a) one notice stack; a failure stays until dismissed or the same operation succeeds, and a failed row keeps its error [notices-and-feedback, services-panel]; (b) a failed Restart Stack stays in the crash view [startup-and-recovery]; (c) each save's outcome stays with its file [config-editor]; (d) a switch's outcome survives its reload [worktree-switch]; (e) a failed chat action reports itself [agent-chat, page-context]. Decision (revision 5, product owner): one outcome per page, in its surface while open, else a notice (`FB-2`), with (a)'s lifetime (`FB-6`, updated by `O53`) and (b) to (e) through the operation record (`ACT-1`, `UNDO-8`); `O51` removed editor-open failures from component-source. Reason: a record on the server outlives any component, which today's per-component state does not (`packages/devhost-ui/src/devtools/features/serviceStatusPanel/hooks/useServiceHealth.ts:55-90`); revision 4 had listed editor-open failures under component-source, which `O51` dropped completely. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, startup-and-recovery, config-editor, worktree-switch, agent-chat, page-context. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (the Disable Reloads result stays in its notice slot, `FB-5`), proxy-settings (a devhost config file change reports in each affected stack's terminal, and on pages whose URL it moved), component-source (copying a source path confirms in the notice stack; editor launching is removed by `O51`). Contract: none; TB-07, CFG-04, WT-02.
- **feedback-04 · What a success or ready report certifies.** Options: (a) resolve each save from the reload that read it, never "applied" while a restarted service is down [config-editor]; (b) `devhost stop` exits 0 only when nothing remains, and no URL is ready while an older process answers it [stack-run-stop]; (c) a session prints ready only once its port answers [serve-folder, startup-and-recovery]; (d) a listener change is checked before it is reported [proxy-settings, manifest-hot-reload]; (e) a switch lands on a working app or says why not [worktree-switch]. Decision: all, `FB-5`, `ACT-11`. Reason: `docker compose up --wait`, `kubectl rollout status` and Vite's ready line certify the end state, not a step. Must change: config-editor, stack-run-stop, serve-folder, startup-and-recovery, proxy-settings, manifest-hot-reload, worktree-switch. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (a restart succeeds when its health check passes), first-run-setup (`devhost caddy start` succeeds once Caddy listens), agent-chat (a Send never claims an unconfirmed delivery). Contract: none; CFG-04, STK-04, SRV-04, SET-04, SET-08.
- **feedback-05 · Partial results of multi-target operations.** Options: (a) a failing, unchanged service never fails Restart Stack, its rollback or a reload [services-panel, manifest-hot-reload]; (b) counts cover the whole stack and results say "of how many" [services-panel]; (c) a failed Restart Stack names the failed service and how many run [startup-and-recovery]; (d) stop and multi-stack commands report each target and exit non-zero [stack-run-stop]; (e) a partly failed save or switch reports per service and keeps the failing step in view, with Undo save [config-editor, worktree-switch]. Decision: all, `FAIL-1`, `UNDO-1`, `REV-11`, `ACT-6`, without Undo save (`UNDO-6`). Reason: the owner's recovery contract; `error-pages-01`, reproduced by its reviewer and its verifier; `docker compose up` reports each container. Must change: services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery, stack-run-stop, config-editor, worktree-switch. Silent, must adopt: proxy-settings (a devhost config file change reports per stack whose URLs moved). Contract: none; SVC-04, MAN-03, STK-04, STK-05, STK-07, WT-02.
- **feedback-06 · A deliberate stop, cancel or replacement is not a failure.** Options: (a) devhost's own stops end with one summary line, never exits awaiting a restart [stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, manifest-hot-reload]; (b) a replaced foreground stack prints a handoff and exits 0 or a documented code, a Ctrl-C of a detached start is a cancellation, a forced stop reads force-stopped [stack-run-stop]; (c) an exit by signal names the signal, the time and the sender [stack-run-stop, services-panel]; (d) a stop by the person is a neutral outcome [agent-chat]. Decision: all, `FAIL-5`, `FAIL-3`, with exit 0 for a replaced stack. Reason: systemd and JetBrains tell a signal from a failure, and Claude Code marks an interrupted turn as interrupted; exit 0 reports a replacement that worked, while `143` stays a person's `SIGTERM`. Must change: stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, manifest-hot-reload, services-panel, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: running-agents (Watch Live Activity shows a stopped turn as stopped), first-run-setup (each stack's terminal tells `devhost caddy stop` from a Caddy crash, `ACT-10`). Contract: `CC-22`, `CC-24`; STK-07, STK-09, CHAT-04.
- **feedback-07 · What a failure report contains.** Options: (a) no markup; an unreadable answer becomes a sentence with the command that fixes it [notices-and-feedback, services-panel, config-editor]; (b) file, line, column and key, the offending line, a close key, and that the previous manifest runs [manifest-hot-reload, stack-run-stop, config-editor]; (c) a process failure's evidence [manifest-hot-reload, stack-run-stop]; (d) structured reasons with **Copy Error** [worktree-switch]; (e) a plain sentence, the raw text as a detail [startup-and-recovery, first-run-setup, agent-chat]; (f) a refusal names what to act on [serve-folder, component-source, third-party-devtools]. Decision: all, `FAIL-2`, `FAIL-3`, `MENU-4`. Reason: dedupe found one missing prerequisite behind six findings, the structured error; compilers and the Vite overlay show the offending line. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, config-editor, manifest-hot-reload, stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, startup-and-recovery, first-run-setup, agent-chat, serve-folder, component-source, third-party-devtools. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (a failed Disable Reloads request says why in its notice slot, `FB-5`). Contract: none; MAN-02, MAN-04, CFG-06, REC-02, WT-05.
- **feedback-08 · The recovery action a failure leads with.** Options: as `selection-12`, plus (h) every recovery the terminal names exists as a command [stack-run-stop] and (i) a failure notice carries its next steps, with the start command on a stopped stack [notices-and-feedback]. Decision: `MENU-5`, `SURF-16`, `MENU-6`, `FAIL-2` (see `selection-12`), with (h) and (i). Reason: as `selection-12`; Docker Desktop offers Restart only where a restart can help. Must change: as `selection-12`, plus stack-run-stop (`devhost service restart`) and notices-and-feedback (a stopped stack leads with its start command, `HOLD-2`). Silent, must adopt: log-minimap (a failure's **Logs** opens the pinned log panel on that service at its exit, `SURF-13`). Contract: `CC-4`, `CC-11`; REC-02, REC-04, REC-05.
- **feedback-09 · Condition notices: when they clear, what dismissal does, what stays when hidden.** Options: (a) collapsing never hides notices, and the badge reflects every condition [toolbar-shell]; (b) a dismissed **Reload blocked** leaves a held marker, it says whether the page is current, and the Disable Reloads request shows pending, then its result [reload-control]; (c) dismissing the hot reload notice is reversible [reload-control]; (d) a rejection clears from another worktree, and a fix replaces it with a confirmation [manifest-hot-reload]; (e) a waiting agent has its own state [agent-chat, running-agents]; (f) a notice an outage raised clears when the outage ends [notices-and-feedback, proxy-settings]. Decision: all, `ACT-7`, `SURF-8`, `UNDO-3`, `FB-5`, `FAIL-4`. Reason: a condition reports a state, so the state's end clears it; VS Code keeps a problems count after a notification is dismissed. Must change: toolbar-shell, reload-control, manifest-hot-reload, agent-chat, running-agents, notices-and-feedback, proxy-settings. Silent, must adopt: config-editor (the Config button counts drafts and shows a rejection while the window is closed, `UNDO-2`), services-panel (the changed mark stays beside an error, `ACT-8`). Contract: `CC-20`; RLD-01, RLD-02, CFG-06, TB-02.
- **feedback-10 · Several reports at once.** Options: (a) one notice stack at the dock edge, at most three plus "N more", never covered [notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell]; (b) docked chat takes the agent notices into a status region of its own [agent-chat, reload-control]; (c) the Configuration window ends above the stack and shows outcomes about its file inside [config-editor]; (d) over a page's modal the notices move into its top layer [toolbar-shell, page-context]. Decision: (a), (c) and (d), and (b)'s other form, chat beside the stack (`SURF-1`); one order for every report, `FB-8`, `FB-2`, `SURF-11`. Reason: one stack keeps one order for everything that needs the person, which a second region inside chat would split; VS Code shows at most three notifications. Must change: notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell, agent-chat, reload-control, config-editor, page-context. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (its results enter the stack in `FB-8`'s order), worktree-switch (its outcome and the agent notices it raises, the same), manifest-hot-reload (its rejection ranks with failures). Contract: `CC-1`, `CC-3`; TB-06, TB-07.
- **feedback-11 · The lost connection and the stopped stack.** Options: (a) a stopping state, then a gray "Disconnected" chip instead of the dots, with the cause and its command [notices-and-feedback]; (b) the last known dots hollow, "reconnecting…" after about 1.5 s, and Restart Stack keeps the stream [services-panel]; (c) writes disabled with a reason [notices-and-feedback, services-panel, config-editor]; (d) pages learn a new origin first [proxy-settings]; (e) terminals say when the proxy is lost and back [stack-run-stop, first-run-setup]; (f) a stopped stack leaves a record [stack-run-stop, stack-directory]. Decision: (b)'s last known state drawn as unknown, without its delay, with (a)'s stopping state and cause and (c) to (f); `ACT-5`, `ACT-3`, `ACT-10`, `HOLD-2`, `O15`. Reason: people decide from the last known state, as VS Code Remote and Kubernetes' Unknown keep it; `HOLD-2` removes Restart Stack's drop at its source, so no unmeasured delay is needed. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, config-editor, proxy-settings, stack-run-stop, first-run-setup, stack-directory. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (its send refusal names the lost connection; ended conversations stay listed, `FB-4`), worktree-switch (its own page reload is not a lost connection), log-minimap (lines keep their times while disconnected), running-agents (says the stack stopped, `ACT-10`). Contract: `CC-26`, `CC-27`; TB-07, STK-04, STK-08.
- **feedback-12 · Rollback and the way back to what runs.** Options: (a) every reload reports, a fix replaces the rejection, a repeated rejection prints one line, a rollback announces itself [manifest-hot-reload]; (b) Rejected is a state of the file, with Restore Running Version, Undo save, Compare, Keep Mine and Use Disk Version [config-editor]; (c) the directory shows a rejected edit as not applied [stack-directory]; (d) a rollback restores what it found [services-panel, manifest-hot-reload]; (e) a failed switch restores and says so [worktree-switch]; (f) local undo restores what the person lost [reload-control, page-context]. Decision: all but Undo save, `UNDO-1`, `UNDO-3`, `UNDO-6`, `UNDO-7`, `FAIL-1`, `FAIL-4`. Reason: Terraform and Kubernetes report a rollback as an outcome of its own; Undo save would hide the second reload it causes (`UNDO-6`). Must change: manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, stack-directory, services-panel, worktree-switch, reload-control, page-context. Silent, must adopt: proxy-settings (a rejected devhost config file keeps the running settings, and its fix reports them restored, `FAIL-4`), agent-chat (the way back from a live edit is the reverse of the agent's own edits, never a whole-file restore, `UNDO-5`, `O22`). Contract: `CC-19`, `CC-20`; MAN-04, CFG-04, CFG-05, WT-02, RLD-02, CTX-02.
- **feedback-13 · Outcomes that name the work they ended or discarded.** Options: (a) `devhost stop` names the conversations it ends, kept resumable [stack-run-stop, agent-chat]; (b) a switch that leaves a conversation behind shows a notice until it is answered [worktree-switch, agent-chat, running-agents]; (c) chat turned off names running conversations, and a harness change starts a new conversation visibly [agent-chat, manifest-hot-reload]; (d) a mark lost on reload stays listed as lost [page-context]; (e) drafts survive, and a save that turns the editor off names the setting that brings it back [config-editor]. Decision: all, `FB-4`, with the question before such writes in `REV-3`; `HOLD-5`, `UNDO-2`, `UNDO-4`, `SEL-8`. Reason: Docker Desktop names what `compose down` removed, and the outcome is the only report a write made outside devhost's UI can give. Must change: stack-run-stop, agent-chat, worktree-switch, running-agents, manifest-hot-reload, page-context, config-editor. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (a restart of the page's own service and Restart Stack name the held reload and page state the page reload discarded), reload-control (a reload that lets held changes through says so). Contract: `CC-18`, `CC-19`; CHAT-05, CHAT-12, CTX-02, CFG-05.
- **feedback-14 · How outcomes are announced to assistive technology.** Options: (a) one polite announcer, each report once, only danger notices as alerts [notices-and-feedback]; (b) a restart keeps focus and announces its start and result once, and the crash view carries its cause and returns focus [services-panel, startup-and-recovery]; (c) status pages read their heading first and announce a failed restart once [startup-and-recovery]; (d) an agent that starts waiting, and each tool call's result, are announced once [agent-chat, running-agents]. Decision (revision 2): all, `FB-9`, `SURF-3`, `ACT-7`, with (a)'s alerts spoken by the announcer's assertive region, no live role on the notices themselves, and the end of each chat turn announced too. Reason: the WAI-ARIA status and alert roles and WCAG 4.1.3; revision 1 also gave every notice a live role, so each report was spoken twice. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, startup-and-recovery, agent-chat, running-agents. Silent, must adopt: config-editor (save results and rejections in the window go through the announcer), worktree-switch (the outcome on the reloaded page is announced once), manifest-hot-reload (a rejection's start and end are announced), stack-directory (status changes are announced politely). Contract: none; TB-05, REC-01, REC-02, REC-03.
- **feedback-15 · Terminal progress and outcome lines (human output).** Options: (a) start prints each URL when published and then a ready line; a detached start names the stack within 1 s and keeps one status line; stop and Ctrl-C name what they wait for, with the time left and the second Ctrl-C [stack-run-stop]; (b) deltas, not inventories, and one completion line per page-started restart [stack-run-stop, services-panel]; (c) every reload or switch ends with one outcome line, and a superseded reload prints one line [manifest-hot-reload, worktree-switch]; (d) setup and serve print versions and next steps, not paths [first-run-setup, serve-folder]; (e) a chat turn writes at most two human lines [agent-chat]. Decision: all, `FB-10`, `FB-3`, `REV-12`. Reason: Vite's ready line, `docker compose up --wait` and Overmind's per-process exit lines. Must change: stack-run-stop, services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, worktree-switch, first-run-setup, serve-folder, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: proxy-settings (a devhost config file change prints what it changed in each stack's terminal), config-editor (a save from the window prints the same outcome line as any save). Contract: `CC-22`; STK-01, STK-02, STK-04, STK-09, SET-02, SET-04, SRV-01, LOG-03.
- **feedback-16 · Where an agent reads an operation's outcome.** Options: (a) one `devhost: ERR:` line per error, marked failures in the stack's log, and `devhost stack info [--json] [--wait]` with short alias `devhost status` (`O62`) [stack-run-stop]; (b) `devhost reload` applies the manifest and waits for the outcome [manifest-hot-reload]; (c) a short 503 for clients that are not browsers [startup-and-recovery]; (d) `devhost logs [-f]`, one format per log file, and chat lines that keep every field for agents [stack-run-stop, agent-chat]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): all, `FB-11`, `FB-7`, `SURF-16`, `O62` (extending shipped `devhost stack info` and adding `devhost status` as its short alias sharing one implementation and output contract). Reason: `docker compose ps --format json`, `tilt wait` and `kubectl wait` give scripts a waitable, exit-coded outcome; human and agent output carry the same information (owner rule). Must change: stack-run-stop, manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (the restart routes answer with documented codes and the operation's outcome, `MENU-6`), worktree-switch (a switch's outcome reaches `devhost stack info` / `devhost status` and the log), config-editor (a save through the config API returns its reload's outcome). Contract: `CC-21`, `CC-23`; STK-06, STK-09, MAN-03, LOG-03.

### 3.4 surfaces

- **surfaces-01 · Toolbar-corner layout order.** Options: (a) the notice stack is never covered: toolbar panels, docked chat and the Configuration window end above it [notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell, config-editor]; (b) while chat is open, the notices about agents (the hot reload notice, **Reload blocked**) move into chat's status region [agent-chat, reload-control]; (c) the running agents list opens above docked chat or in its header [running-agents]; (d) the dock moves onto the top edge of a bottom-docked library panel [third-party-devtools]. Decision: (a), (c)'s first form and (d), `SURF-1`. Reason: each notice keeps one place in every state; (b) would give the hot reload notice two homes and move the anchor of `O2`'s popover, while (a) already keeps Disable Reloads usable with chat open; VS Code's panels never overlap one another or the status bar. Must change: agent-chat (chat docks beyond the notice stack), running-agents (the list paints above chat), third-party-devtools (the dock moves), config-editor (the window's height is capped), notices-and-feedback and toolbar-shell (the corner layout). Silent, must adopt: services-panel and worktree-switch (their panels follow `SURF-1`), log-minimap (the strip and the pinned log panel take `SURF-5`'s edge order), component-source (states the source menu's place in the order), page-context (selection highlights and drawings stay beneath devhost's surfaces). Contract: `CC-1`; TB-06.
- **surfaces-02 · Crash view surface and modality.** Options: (a) one recovery screen per failure and a deterministic Escape [startup-and-recovery]; (b) the view minimizes by a control and by Escape to a banner above the toolbar so Config, Services and Chat respond, with Edit beside Restart [startup-and-recovery, config-editor, services-panel]; (c) the view stays where a restart started from it reports, stays open when Restart Stack fails, carries the cause as its description and returns focus when it closes [services-panel, startup-and-recovery, notices-and-feedback]. Decision (revision 2): all three, `SURF-3`: the banner is a crash notice in the notice stack, and Escape always minimizes and never dismisses, which makes it deterministic; the view opens minimized while the person types or chat is in use, and chat and the notices stay operable over it. Reason: a page whose service exited cannot work, so a modal is honest, but fixing the cause needs Config, Services and Chat, and in **This worktree (live)** an agent's edit is the expected cause of the exit (`O1`); the Next.js dev overlay collapses into a badge that reopens it; the current Escape closes the dialog once per user activation and leaves it drawn (`packages/devhost-ui/src/devtools/features/serviceStatusPanel/components/ServiceCrashOverlay.tsx:48`). Must change: startup-and-recovery, config-editor (Edit from the crash view), services-panel, notices-and-feedback (the crash notice). Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (chat stays operable over the crash view, which opens minimized while chat is in use), manifest-hot-reload (a fix saved while the view is open reports in it), log-minimap (the view and the pinned log panel show the same lines), worktree-switch (a switch started from the view reports in it). Contract: `CC-2`; REC-03.
- **surfaces-03 · devhost's full page versus the app's own response.** Options: (a) an HTML 5xx from the app shows the app's own document under a compact devhost banner [startup-and-recovery]; (b) a 404 stays a devhost page that names who answered, with the app's body collapsed [startup-and-recovery, worktree-switch]; (c) devhost's file server is exempt and shows its own 404 in the file browser [serve-folder, startup-and-recovery]; (d) clients that are not browsers get a short devhost status instead of a status page or an empty 502 [startup-and-recovery, stack-run-stop]. Decision: (a) for 404s and 5xxs alike, (c) and (d), `SURF-16`, settled by `O5` (decided 2026-11-18). Reason: see `selection-12`; Caddy and Traefik pass upstream error bodies through unless an error handler is configured. Must change: startup-and-recovery, worktree-switch (the worktree list collapsed in the notice), serve-folder, stack-run-stop (scripts get a 503 with `Retry-After`). Silent, must adopt: stack-directory (the Caddy 404 is devhost's own answer and stays a devhost page, `SEL-10`). Contract: `CC-4`; REC-04, REC-05.
- **surfaces-04 · Configuration window modality.** Options: (a) a true modal dialog: `aria-modal`, the page and the toolbar inert, focus cycling inside and back on Config after closing [config-editor]; (b) a modal window that ends above the notice stack, whose buttons keep working while it is open [toolbar-shell, config-editor]; (c) the window carries the context it was opened for: a rejected reload or an exited service as a banner with marked lines [config-editor, manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery, services-panel]. Decision: (a) and (c), with (b)'s geometry but without operable notices, `SURF-4`. Reason: the WAI-ARIA dialog pattern makes everything outside a modal inert; once the window carries the rejection and the exited service itself, nothing the person needs while editing is behind it, and the notices stay visible so nothing arrives unseen. Must change: config-editor (modal semantics, height cap and context banner; `config-editor-11`'s requirement that notice buttons work while the window is open becomes that the notices stay visible), toolbar-shell (the notice stack visible behind the window), manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery and services-panel (Edit opens the window with its context). Silent, must adopt: proxy-settings (a rejected devhost config file change shows the same banner in the window). Contract: none; CFG-01, CFG-06.
- **surfaces-05 · Docked chat panel geometry.** Options: (a) the composer and Send stay inside the viewport in every state, a moved or expanded panel re-fits, and a pending approval or question is a card pinned between the conversation and the composer [agent-chat, page-context]. Decision: (a), `SURF-6`, `SURF-5`. Reason: Cursor and VS Code pin chat's input at every size; Claude Code shows a pending permission in place of its input. Must change: agent-chat, page-context (the decision card). Silent, must adopt: reload-control (the Disable Reloads popover stays inside the viewport with chat docked), running-agents (its list above chat, `SURF-1`), toolbar-shell (the dock and chat in `SURF-5`'s edge order). Contract: none; CHAT-02, CHAT-06.
- **surfaces-06 · What a collapsed or dismissed surface still shows.** Options: (a) collapsing hides segments, never pending notices, and the collapsed badge reflects every notice [toolbar-shell, reload-control, manifest-hot-reload]; (b) the Chat button and the running agents trigger show a needs-you state [agent-chat, running-agents]; (c) a dismissed **Reload blocked** leaves a held marker with a count [reload-control]; (d) Watch Live Activity shows an agent waiting for approval [running-agents]. Decision: all, `SURF-8`; the attention state itself is 2.10's. Reason: VS Code badges a collapsed view that needs attention and keeps the notification dot while toasts are hidden. Must change: toolbar-shell, reload-control, manifest-hot-reload, agent-chat, running-agents. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (failed and changed services reach the collapsed badge), notices-and-feedback (every condition notice has a collapsed form), worktree-switch (a blocked or unavailable worktree reaches the collapsed badge), config-editor (a rejected save keeps its attention mark on **Config** until a reload is accepted). Contract: none; TB-02, RLD-02, AGT-01.
- **surfaces-07 · Toolbar placement and who chooses it.** Options: (a) a per-person placement with the manifest as the default, by drag or a keyboard-reachable **Move to**, saved per project and origin in the browser settings store [toolbar-shell]; (b) chat's dragged position is page layout saved on the server per manifest, and a moved panel keeps off the toolbar's row [agent-chat]; (c) devhost moves the toolbar above a docked library panel by itself [third-party-devtools]. Decision: (a)'s per-person choice with (b)'s storage, and (c) as a temporary move that never changes the saved choice, `SURF-7`, `SURF-1`. Reason: the contract keeps chat's position on the server because browser storage is separate per hostname, and a stack serves several hostnames, so (a)'s storage would move the toolbar between them; the Next.js dev indicator, TanStack Devtools and the Vercel toolbar let each person place theirs. Must change: toolbar-shell (storage on the control server), agent-chat (a moved panel stays off the bar's row), third-party-devtools. Silent, must adopt: log-minimap (the pinned log panel's width is saved like chat's position), page-context (the drawing tools follow the dock's corner), running-agents (the list follows the dock). Contract: `CC-5`; TB-02, CHAT-02.
- **surfaces-08 · When a toolbar popover outgrows a popover.** Options: (a) above about 8 services the trigger becomes a summary that never wraps the bar, and above about 10 the panel gains a filter, groups, a pinned attention group and a sticky footer [services-panel, toolbar-shell]; (b) the in-use popover shows one Open Stack with the other routes under a disclosure [worktree-switch]. Decision: (a) with a fit test instead of the counts, and (b), `SURF-10`, `BAR-1`. Reason: no measurement supports 8 or 10, and whether dots wrap depends on the window's width, so the test is whether they fit; Docker Desktop and Tilt group resources and keep stack actions out of the scrolling list. Must change: services-panel, toolbar-shell, worktree-switch. Silent, must adopt: running-agents (its list overflows per `SURF-10`), third-party-devtools (launchers collapse into one overflow control when they would wrap the bar). Contract: none; TB-03, EXT-01.
- **surfaces-09 · Viewport containment and acceptance viewports.** Options: (a) a surface's header actions stay in view, an unreadable body becomes one sentence, notices wrap long paths, completions open inside the window, and checks run at 1600x1000, 1280x800 and 900x900 [notices-and-feedback, config-editor, services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, agent-chat, third-party-devtools, toolbar-shell]. Decision: (a), `SURF-9`; narrower windows and phone layouts for chat, approvals and notices are settled by `O8` (decided 2026-11-18). Reason: those three viewports are the ones every finding measured, plus 640x400 and 320 CSS pixels for `O8`. Must change: notices-and-feedback, config-editor, services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, agent-chat, third-party-devtools, toolbar-shell. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch, running-agents, page-context, log-minimap (each checks its surfaces at the acceptance viewports). Contract: none.
- **surfaces-10 · Coexistence with the host page.** Options: (a) only the bar, notice cards and open panels take pointer input [toolbar-shell, page-context]; (b) under the page's modal dialog the notice stack moves into the modal's top layer and the rest of the toolbar shows an explicit paused state, a page popover never covers an open devtools panel, and `guides/devtools.md` is corrected [toolbar-shell, reload-control]. Decision: (a) and (b), `SURF-11`, `MENU-3`. Reason: the WAI-ARIA modal pattern makes controls outside a page's modal inert, which promotion cannot change (`packages/devhost-ui/src/devtools/AGENTS.md`: "promotion changes paint order only"), so the toolbar says it is paused instead of drawing dead controls. Must change: toolbar-shell, page-context, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (chat keeps working inside the page's modal, as today), component-source (the source menu takes input only where it draws), log-minimap (the strip is the only page input it takes), services-panel (its panel shows the paused state under the page's modal). Contract: `CC-3`; TB-06.
- **surfaces-11 · Overlays drawn on page content.** Options: (a) drawings anchor to the element they mark, and source-highlight outlines follow their elements through scroll, resize and DOM changes [page-context, component-source]. Decision: (a), `SURF-12`. Reason: browser DevTools' highlight follows its element through layout changes. Must change: page-context, component-source. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (sent context names each mark's element and flags a mark whose element is gone, `SEL-9`), reload-control (states that a hot update keeps marks anchored). Contract: none; CTX-02, SRC-02.
- **surfaces-12 · Where service logs are read in the browser.** Options: (a) the hover preview stays a peek and a pinned log panel docks at the right edge [log-minimap]; (b) a rejected reload's notice keeps the failing service's output [manifest-hot-reload, config-editor]; (c) the crash view and the recovery page open their logs at the end with the exit summary pinned, from one shared view [startup-and-recovery]; (d) chat activity is summarized per turn and labelled by conversation [agent-chat]. Decision: all, `SURF-13`, `SURF-5`, `SURF-14`; (d)'s content stays agent-chat's. Reason: Tilt and Docker Desktop keep a per-resource log pane with search and copy; VS Code's Output panel keeps the selection. Must change: log-minimap, manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, startup-and-recovery, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (a row opens the pinned log panel filtered to its service), stack-run-stop (states where a detached stack's full log lives, 2.7). Contract: none; LOG-01, LOG-02, LOG-03.
- **surfaces-13 · Surface for an in-place confirmation.** Options: (a) a popover anchored to the hot reload notice, per owner decision q5 [reload-control]; (b) **Restart Stack** expands in place in the Services panel [services-panel]; (c) **Stop Stack** confirms in place in the in-use popover, which stays open with progress [worktree-switch]; (d) chat's running-agent prompt stays inline in the panel [agent-chat]. Decision: all, `SURF-2`; whether to ask, and the question's content, default and keys, are 2.5's. Reason: every position keeps the question beside its control and none uses a modal dialog; Docker Desktop and VS Code tie a confirmation to its action. Must change: reload-control, services-panel, worktree-switch, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: stack-run-stop and first-run-setup (a terminal question is a prompt in the terminal, 2.5), config-editor (a confirmation inside the window expands in place there). Contract: none; RLD-01, SVC-04, WT-03, CHAT-07.
- **surfaces-14 · One component for a state on both page types.** Options: (a) one worktree list component on status pages and in the toolbar [worktree-switch]; (b) one shared recovery view for the crash view and the recovery page [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: (a) and (b), `SURF-14`. Reason: the same state worded and drawn two ways is the inconsistency the findings report; the two page types keep separate stylesheets by contract (`packages/devhost-ui/AGENTS.md`), so the shared component ships in both. Must change: worktree-switch, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: stack-directory (cards draw service states with the shared state component), services-panel (its rows use it), serve-folder (file views share the status pages' components and words). Contract: none; REC-02, REC-03, WT-06.
- **surfaces-15 · Hand-offs between surfaces.** Options: (a) start output and `stack list` print the stack directory's URL, which the toolbar's stack name menu and the 404 link [stack-run-stop, stack-directory]; (b) running agents offer Open Chat for chat's agent, and Watch Live Activity stays on 127.0.0.1 in its own tab [running-agents]; (c) Edit and Open in Editor go to the file and line of the problem [config-editor, manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery]; (d) a service row links only to an address that answers [services-panel]; (e) the terminal gets `devhost service` commands [stack-run-stop]. Decision (revision 5, product owner): (a), (d), (e), (b) extended so Watch Live Activity also opens on the stack directory's hostname for paired browsers (`O55`, decided 2026-11-18), and (c) as **Edit** only (**Open in Editor** removed by `O51`, 2026-11-18); `SURF-15`, `MENU-4`, `MENU-6`. Reason: owner rules (devhost never opens a browser; `HOLD-8` opens Watch Live Activity to paired browsers on the stack directory's hostname while keeping it off app hostnames; `O51` drops editor support completely); Tilt prints its UI's URL at start; revision 4 kept Open in Editor and loopback-only Watch Live Activity. Must change: stack-run-stop, stack-directory, running-agents, config-editor, manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery, services-panel. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (after its page reload, the page names the worktree it now runs, and the terminal names both worktrees, `SEL-4`), log-minimap (the pinned log panel says where the full log lives), agent-chat (states how chat hands off to the running agents list and Watch Live Activity). Contract: none; REC-07, AGT-02.

### 3.5 keyboard

- **keyboard-01 · Restart shortcut: scope, target, suspension and feedback.** Options: (a) stays page-wide and suspended in text entry, names its target and outcome, and an ignored press says why [services-panel]; (b) restarts the page's service when nothing changed [services-panel]; (c) never targets a reverted edit [reload-control, services-panel]; (d) a failure outlives the next health message, and the chord is unavailable with its reason while disconnected [notices-and-feedback, services-panel]. Decision: all, `KEY-1`, `KEY-2`, `KEY-11`, `SEL-2` ((b) settled by `O4`), `ACT-8`, `FB-6`, `HOLD-2`; the chord is suspended while a devhost modal or menu is open, so it no longer fires behind the crash view. Reason: VS Code and JetBrains scope a restart key by context and report its result; `toolbar-shell-08` shows documented key names that never fire. Must change: services-panel (the chord's outcome and its reason when unavailable), reload-control (a reverted edit clears the mark), toolbar-shell (configured chords reported, `KEY-2`). Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery (the chord is suspended under the crash view), worktree-switch (the chord is unavailable during a switch, with its reason). Contract: `CC-6`, `CC-29`; SVC-03, TB-05.
- **keyboard-02 · Modifier family for devhost bindings and collisions with page and third-party keys.** Options: (a) a focus chord Alt+Ctrl+D [toolbar-shell]; (b) Alt+Shift+D [toolbar-shell]; (c) a log chord Alt+Shift+L [log-minimap]; (d) Mod+Z and Mod+Shift+Z page-wide while annotating [page-context]; (e) Alt + right-click and Alt-hold kept, the browser menu taken only when devhost shows something [component-source, page-context]. Decision: one Alt+Ctrl family on the physical key, with Alt+Ctrl+B and Alt+Ctrl+O instead of (a) to (c), `KEY-2`; (d) only in a mode or with focus in devhost, `KEY-12`; (e), with `MENU-3` always showing something, and no chord starting selection. Reason: Alt+Shift+D is Vue DevTools' dock key, common Linux desktops reserve Ctrl+Alt+D and Ctrl+Alt+L, and VoiceOver's modifier is Control+Option, so no action is chord-only; revision 2 adds Windows: Magnifier leaves B and O free and takes R, which stays documented and rebindable (`O17`). Must change: toolbar-shell, log-minimap, page-context, component-source. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (its chord stays in the family, configurable), third-party-devtools (devhost binds none of the libraries' keys), agent-chat (Alt-hold in the composer only when Alt is pressed alone), config-editor (CodeMirror's Alt chords stay inside the text). Contract: `CC-29`; TB-05, CTX-01, SRC-01.
- **keyboard-03 · Reaching devhost from the page: focus shortcut, landmark and DOM position.** Options: (a) a configurable chord to the toolbar and back, a landmark named devhost, notices before the bar in DOM order where they are drawn above it [toolbar-shell]. Decision: (a), `KEY-3`, `KEY-4`, with the key from keyboard-02. Reason: WAI-ARIA landmarks let screen-reader users jump by region, and browser devtools take focus with their own key. Must change: toolbar-shell. Silent, must adopt: services-panel, worktree-switch, config-editor, agent-chat, running-agents, reload-control, third-party-devtools, manifest-hot-reload, notices-and-feedback (each states how its entry point is reached from the bar and where focus returns, `KEY-6`). Contract: `CC-29`; TB-05.
- **keyboard-04 · Moving between controls inside the toolbar bar.** Options: (a) the toolbar pattern with roving focus [toolbar-shell, services-panel]; (b) readouts as Tab stops [toolbar-shell]; (c) move and collapse operable from the keyboard [toolbar-shell]. Decision: (a), with readouts as arrow stops that open their details instead of (b), and (c) through **Move to** and **Collapse** in the stack name menu, `KEY-4`. Reason: the toolbar pattern is one Tab stop, which (b) would break, and arrow stops keep the readouts reachable. Must change: toolbar-shell. Silent, must adopt: running-agents, reload-control, third-party-devtools, worktree-switch (their triggers are arrow stops that open with Enter, Space or the arrow toward the panel). Contract: none; TB-02, TB-04, TB-05.
- **keyboard-05 · Keyboard isolation between devtools text entry and the host page.** Options: (a) devtools text entry in a same-origin frame so the page's listeners never see its keys, and the docs say exactly what is isolated [toolbar-shell]. Decision (revision 2): (a)'s goal by other means, `KEY-5`: a window capture listener injected before any page script stops those keys, and text entry stays in the Shadow DOM. Reason: `toolbar-shell-06` reproduced a page's capture-phase shortcut receiving keys typed into Find worktree, against the docs' promise; browser devtools run in their own frame for the same reason, which revision 1 followed, but a frame splits a combobox's field from its list and a field from what names it, and ARIA references cannot cross it. Must change: toolbar-shell. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (the composer), config-editor (the configuration text), worktree-switch (the find field), page-context (the crop fields and the Deny note), stack-directory (its filter sits on devhost's own page, which needs no early listener, but keeps the field's keys to itself). Contract: `CC-28`; TB-05.
- **keyboard-06 · Who owns the keyboard after a pointer interaction with the toolbar.** Options: (a) a non-text control keeps only the keys it uses, and a panel closed after pointer use returns focus to the page element [toolbar-shell]; (b) keep the documented "Click the page to give it the keyboard back" [toolbar-shell]. Decision: (a), `KEY-5`, `KEY-6`. Reason: native popovers and VS Code menus return focus after a pointer dismissal, while today any click on the toolbar silences the page's own keys. Must change: toolbar-shell. Silent, must adopt: services-panel, worktree-switch, running-agents, third-party-devtools, reload-control (their panels return focus per `KEY-6`). Contract: `CC-28`; TB-05.
- **keyboard-07 · Escape on devhost surfaces.** Options: (a) Escape or **Minimize** collapses the crash view [startup-and-recovery]; (b) focus returns whenever the crash view closes [startup-and-recovery, services-panel]; (c) the first Escape in the configuration text moves to the header, the second closes [config-editor]; (d) Escape always closes the window [notices-and-feedback, config-editor]; (e) Escape closes the Worktrees picker and, in the agent warning, returns to the picker [worktree-switch]; (f) Escape never answers an approval, returns from the Deny note and cancels the running-agent prompt [agent-chat]; (g) no devhost Escape on a library's panel [third-party-devtools]; (h) Escape closes the transient surface it belongs to [component-source, log-minimap, services-panel]. Decision: all but (d), which gives way to (c) because the window is modal and keeps its actions in view (`SURF-4`, `SURF-9`); `KEY-8`, `KEY-6`. Reason: VS Code closes only the topmost widget per Escape, and the WAI-ARIA dialog pattern returns focus to the invoker. Must change: startup-and-recovery, config-editor, worktree-switch, agent-chat, component-source, log-minimap, services-panel. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (Escape cancels the Disable Reloads popover), running-agents (its panel closes and returns focus), toolbar-shell (owns the layer order Escape walks), manifest-hot-reload (Escape never dismisses its rejection notice). Contract: `CC-2`, `CC-29`; REC-03, CFG-02, CHAT-04, CHAT-06.
- **keyboard-08 · Escape and Tab inside devhost text entry.** Options: (a) Escape keeps stopping the turn, and stopping is a labelled button away from Send [agent-chat]; (b) Escape keeps leaving selection or drawing mode, its hint reading Esc Finish [page-context]; (c) Tab indents and the first Escape leaves the text [config-editor]. Decision (revision 2): (b) and (c), ordered by `KEY-8`: a mode first, then focus back to the page; (a) in part: Escape never stops a turn, which the labelled **Stop Turn** and its own key do (`KEY-9`); the window's hint names Ctrl+M's Tab toggle (`KEY-13`). Reason: `keyboard-and-screen-reader-06` shows Escape meant to finish selecting also stopping the turn, and revision 1's order still stopped it on the next press, ending a live edit midway; GitHub's web editor and CodeMirror leave the text with Escape, then Tab. Must change: agent-chat, page-context, config-editor. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (in the request, Escape cancels the popover and Tab leaves the text), toolbar-shell (the early listener of `KEY-5` hands these keys to them). Contract: `CC-29`; CHAT-04, CTX-01, CFG-03.
- **keyboard-09 · Enter as the default action: send, switch, confirm, filter.** Options: (a) Enter in Find worktree starts the switch [worktree-switch]; (b) the agent warning keeps focus and Enter on **Answer First** [worktree-switch]; (c) Enter confirms Restart Stack [services-panel]; (d) no key approves, the running-agent prompt defaults to Cancel, and Enter sends the Deny note [agent-chat]; (e) during a turn Send says the message joins the turn [agent-chat]; (f) filters act as typed [stack-directory]. Decision: (b), (d) (including standing approvals, `O12`), (e), (f); (a) picks without switching and (c) takes the safe choice; `KEY-9`, `REV-2`, `REV-4`. Reason: WAI-ARIA alert dialogs focus the least destructive action, and a search field must never start a stack-wide restart; Claude Code and Cursor send on Enter. Must change: worktree-switch, services-panel, agent-chat, stack-directory. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (Enter in the request inserts a line break), page-context (Enter in a crop field never shares), startup-and-recovery (Enter in a status page's worktree find field never switches). Contract: `CC-14`; WT-02, SVC-04, CHAT-03, CHAT-06, CHAT-07, REC-07.
- **keyboard-10 · Focus on open and focus return on close.** Options: (a) focus returns to the trigger or the element focused before, never the body [config-editor, worktree-switch, services-panel, startup-and-recovery, toolbar-shell]; (b) an arriving approval card and the running-agent prompt take focus [agent-chat]; (c) the crash view focuses its summary [startup-and-recovery]; (d) it focuses its primary action [startup-and-recovery]; (e) the picker focuses a search field at its top [worktree-switch]; (f) the source menu takes focus [component-source]. Decision (revision 2): (a), (c) as the view's heading with the cause as its description and the lead action as the next Tab stop, (e) and (f); (b) is rejected; `KEY-6`, `REV-9`, `SURF-3`. Reason: a card that took focus could take keys meant for the composer as an answer, against the owner rule that devhost never answers for the person; revision 1 chose (d), but a view that opens by itself while the person types takes a Space or Enter as Restart, and in **This worktree (live)** an agent's edit is the expected cause of the exit (`O1`). Must change: config-editor, worktree-switch, services-panel, startup-and-recovery, toolbar-shell, agent-chat, component-source. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (focus starts in the request), running-agents (focus to its first row action), third-party-devtools (launchers return focus), notices-and-feedback (no notice takes focus), page-context (Select Elements leaves focus where it was). Contract: none; REC-03, CFG-02, WT-01, CHAT-06, SRC-01.
- **keyboard-11 · Focus containment: modal and non-modal devhost surfaces.** Options: (a) the Configuration window is modal [config-editor]; (b) Tab never leaves the open Worktrees picker [worktree-switch]; (c) Tab leaves the toolbar [toolbar-shell]; (d) the crash view stops trapping once minimized [startup-and-recovery]; (e) under the page's modal, notices move into it and the rest is paused [toolbar-shell, reload-control]. Decision: (a), (c), (d), (e), and (b) as Tab past a panel's last control closing it and returning to its trigger; `KEY-7`, `SURF-4`, `SURF-11`. Reason: the WAI-ARIA patterns trap Tab only in modal dialogs; closing the panel keeps Tab from wandering while it stays open. Must change: config-editor, worktree-switch, toolbar-shell, startup-and-recovery, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: services-panel, running-agents, third-party-devtools (their panels close on Tab past the last control), agent-chat (docked chat is non-modal, and inert while the Configuration window is open), manifest-hot-reload (its notice moves into the page's modal). Contract: `CC-3`; TB-06, CFG-01.
- **keyboard-12 · Busy and disabled controls keep keyboard focus.** Options: (a) `aria-disabled`, focus kept, a "Restarting api…" label and one polite region [services-panel, startup-and-recovery]; (b) after a failure focus moves to the error [startup-and-recovery]; (c) Send and Stop never adjacent [agent-chat]. Decision: (a), (c), and (b) only when the outcome replaces the control; `KEY-11`, `FB-9`. Reason: the authoring practices prefer `aria-disabled` for controls that must stay focusable, and moving focus off a control that still exists loses the person's place. Must change: services-panel, startup-and-recovery, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (**Switch and Restart Stack** keeps focus while it runs), config-editor (Save keeps focus through the reload), notices-and-feedback (a failure becomes the control's description). Contract: none; SVC-02, SVC-04, WT-02, CFG-04, CHAT-04.
- **keyboard-13 · Keyboard model in lists, pickers and menus.** Options: (a) the worktree list as a combobox with its search at the top [worktree-switch]; (b) the source menu with arrows, Enter, and c to copy [component-source]; (c) a focused filter atop an overflowing Services panel [services-panel]; (d) Mod+P opens the File list [config-editor]; (e) typing filters the stack directory [stack-directory]. Decision: (a), (c), (e), and (b) with type-ahead instead of c; (d) is rejected because Mod+P is the browser's Print, and Escape from the text reaches the File list; `KEY-10`. Reason: the WAI-ARIA combobox and menu patterns, and VS Code's quick pick. Must change: worktree-switch, component-source, services-panel, config-editor, stack-directory. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (its agent, model and conversation pickers are comboboxes), running-agents (row actions are Tab stops). Contract: none; WT-01, SRC-01, SVC-01, CFG-02, REC-07.
- **keyboard-14 · Keyboard paths for pointer-only features.** Options: (a) a pinned log panel by click, chord or **Logs** [log-minimap]; (b) readouts reachable and the toolbar movable [toolbar-shell]; (c) Mod+Z undoes page context whenever focus is outside text fields [page-context]; (d) the source menu operable once open [component-source]. Decision (revision 2): (a) with Alt+Ctrl+O, (b), (d), and (c) only in a mode or with focus in devhost; picking an element, opening its source menu and drawing a shape from the keyboard or by two clicks, which no flow proposed, are added; `KEY-12`. Reason: WCAG 2.1.1 exempts only input that depends on the path of a movement; revision 1 exempted Arrow, Line and Box, which depend only on their ends, and 2.5.7 asks a single-pointer way to do what a drag does; Chrome DevTools walks the DOM with the arrows. Must change: log-minimap, toolbar-shell, page-context, component-source. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (its panel hosts these tools; focus returns to the composer after a pick). Contract: `CC-29`; LOG-01, TB-02, TB-04, CTX-01, CTX-02, SRC-01.
- **keyboard-15 · Shortcut hints, notation and one definition per binding.** Options: (a) a key cap followed only by its outcome [page-context]; (b) macOS glyphs [config-editor]; (c) the key in the label, "Stop (Esc)" [agent-chat]; (d) a Keyboard section in the docs [toolbar-shell]; (e) the docs describe the target as the UI shows it [services-panel]. Decision: (a), (b) on macOS with words elsewhere, (d), (e); (c) gives way to `Button`'s `shortcut` prop, which the UI package requires; `KEY-13`, `BAR-4`. Reason: VS Code and JetBrains draw every binding per platform from one registry. Must change: page-context, config-editor, agent-chat, toolbar-shell, services-panel. Silent, must adopt: log-minimap, worktree-switch, component-source, reload-control (their new keys show hints from the same definition). Contract: `CC-29`; TB-05, CFG-04.
- **keyboard-16 · Terminal keys: Ctrl-C and naming it.** Options: (a) name what still stops with a countdown, and force on a second Ctrl-C [stack-run-stop]; (b) a second Ctrl-C during the proxy steps exits within 2 s and says what is left [stack-run-stop]; (c) Ctrl-C during a detached wait is a cancellation [stack-run-stop]; (d) a serve session names Ctrl-C once ready [serve-folder]. Decision (revision 2): all, `KEY-14`, `FB-10`, `FAIL-5`, and `devhost stop` forces a detached stack the same way, on Ctrl-C while it waits or a second `devhost stop`. Reason: Docker Compose names its force step and forces on the second press; revision 1 left a detached stack's waiting stop with no way to force it but `kill -9`. Must change: stack-run-stop, serve-folder. Silent, must adopt: first-run-setup (Ctrl-C during `devhost caddy download` keeps the old binary; at the trust prompt it skips trust and names `devhost caddy trust`). Contract: `CC-22`, `CC-35`; STK-01, STK-02, SRV-01, SET-02, SET-03.

### 3.6 language

- **language-01 · The noun for a running devhost unit: stack, serve session, project, process.** Options: (a) a stack, named by its manifest name with its worktree when two share it, the PID a detail [stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, stack-directory]; (b) a serve session is not a stack [serve-folder]; (c) no "Start a project" [stack-directory]; (d) chat edits "this worktree", not "this project" [agent-chat, reload-control]. Decision: all, `WORD-1`, `WORD-10`, `WORD-11`, `STATE-2`. Reason: `CONTEXT.md` defines Stack and avoids project; the owner unified working copies as worktrees (`WORD-11`). Must change: stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, stack-directory, serve-folder, agent-chat, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: services-panel and startup-and-recovery (say stack, never project), notices-and-feedback (outcomes name the stack), proxy-settings (names the stacks whose URLs move), first-run-setup (help and output say stack). Contract: `CC-32`; STK-04, STK-06, SRV-01, REC-07.
- **language-02 · Configuration nouns: manifest, manifest file, devhost config file, configuration, config.** Options: (a) files named by their glossary role and grouped "This stack" and under the machine name ("devbox", `O61`), with a line that the devhost config file reaches every stack [config-editor]; (b) "Shared Caddy settings" [proxy-settings]; (c) "manifest" for the stack's configuration [manifest-hot-reload]. Decision: all, `WORD-1`; "configuration" names both kinds of file together, so **Config** and the Configuration window keep their names. Reason: the glossary lacks a word for both kinds together, and VS Code labels settings by scope. Must change: config-editor, proxy-settings, manifest-hot-reload. Silent, must adopt: stack-run-stop (the terminal says manifest and devhost config file), notices-and-feedback (a rejection notice names the file's kind), stack-directory (names the kinds of file it lists), toolbar-shell (Config's tooltip), startup-and-recovery (points to the manifest file and line). Contract: `CC-32`; CFG-02, SET-08, MAN-04.
- **language-03 · Hot reload names two mechanisms: applying a manifest and the app's framework reloads.** Options: (a) hot reload is applying a saved manifest, and the app's HMR is another concept [manifest-hot-reload, config-editor]; (b) **Disable Reloads** keeps its label and its request is written for the framework [reload-control]; (c) the owner's q4 wording calls the app's reloads hot reload [agent-chat]. Decision: (a) and (b): visible text calls the app's mechanism reloads, `WORD-1`, `WORD-10`; the reference keeps q5's name, the hot reload notice, for the component, whose title names what the app does. Reason: `CONTEXT.md` defines Hot reload; q5 keeps **Disable Reloads** and **Hold Reloads**. Must change: manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, reload-control, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: notices-and-feedback and toolbar-shell (both notices side by side, worded this way). Contract: `CC-31`, `CC-32`; RLD-01, RLD-02, MAN-03.
- **language-04 · Worktree vocabulary, and the label that identifies a worktree.** Options: (a) the branch or "Detached <SHA>" with the `~` path everywhere [worktree-switch]; (b) "detached at <SHA>" with the folder [stack-directory]; (c) separate terms for what the page runs and a new linked worktree [agent-chat, reload-control]; (d) "worktree root" instead of "." [running-agents]. Decision (revision 5, product owner): (a), (d) and `WORD-11` (`SEL-4`, `WORD-1`): worktree names every working copy, main's included, and never "checkout" as a noun; (b) takes (a)'s label and (c)'s split is withdrawn. Reason: `git worktree list` and GitHub Desktop label by branch with the path second, and one label avoids two detached spellings; revision 4 kept (c)'s split between "checkout" and "worktree", which the product owner rejected on 2026-11-18 ("why do we need checkouts?", owner item q17). Must change: worktree-switch, stack-directory, agent-chat, reload-control, running-agents. Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery, services-panel, notices-and-feedback, component-source (each names worktrees by the `SEL-4` label). Contract: `CC-31`, `CC-32`; WT-01, WT-05, WT-06.
- **language-05 · Chat nouns: conversation or session, harness or agent, and who wrote a transcript entry.** Options: (a) conversation everywhere, titled by its first message [agent-chat]; (b) product names, never IDs or native statuses [agent-chat, running-agents]; (c) the Disable Reloads popover names the agent [reload-control]; (d) only the person's messages are theirs [agent-chat]; (e) readable entry words in Watch Live Activity [running-agents]. Decision: all, with **Agent** replacing **Harness** in visible text; `WORD-1`, `WORD-10`. Reason: Cursor, Claude Code and ChatGPT list chats by title, and the owner's q5 names "the agent". Must change: agent-chat, running-agents, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: page-context (calls the recipient the agent), toolbar-shell (Chat's accessible name uses `WORD-6`'s words), log-minimap (chat lines name the conversation's title). Contract: `CC-31`, `CC-32`; CHAT-01, CHAT-05, AGT-02.
- **language-06 · Internal identifiers shown where people need names.** Options: (a) conversations by first message [agent-chat]; (b) agents by product name, "worktree root" and where they run [running-agents]; (c) worktrees by branch, stacks by name and worktree [worktree-switch, stack-directory]; (d) a held hostname names its holder in the person's terms [serve-folder]; (e) stop output names the stack, and collisions the owning stack [stack-run-stop, proxy-settings]; (f) launchers name the library and the instance [third-party-devtools]. Decision: all, `WORD-4`, `WORD-9`; agent output keeps the identifiers. Reason: Docker Desktop lists by name with the ID second. Must change: agent-chat, running-agents, worktree-switch, stack-directory, serve-folder, stack-run-stop, proxy-settings, third-party-devtools. Silent, must adopt: config-editor (files by role and short path), notices-and-feedback (outcomes name their targets), log-minimap (services by name). Contract: none; CHAT-05, AGT-01, STK-04, EXT-04.
- **language-07 · One name per operation across toolbar, pages, terminal and CLI.** Options: (a) `devhost service restart|start|stop` [stack-run-stop]; (b) Restart Changed labelled by its target [services-panel]; (c) **Switch and Restart Stack** on every surface [worktree-switch]; (d) a replaced stack says it was replaced [stack-run-stop]; (e) **Deny**, **Allow Once** and **Stop Turn** beside the composer's Stop Generating [agent-chat]; (f) recovery pages hand off to the Services panel's restart [startup-and-recovery, services-panel]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): all, `WORD-2`, `MENU-6`: Stop Generating becomes **Stop Turn**; under `O18` (decided 2026-11-25, owner item q79a2), devhost adds `devhost upgrade` and keeps shipped `devhost restart-all` and project-scoped `devhost stack restart` (withdrawing revision 5's `devhost stack replace` rename while stating clearly in help and output that they replace the running devhost supervisor process). Reason: `docker compose` and Docker Desktop use one verb for one target; keeping `restart-all` and `stack restart` on one verb avoids breaking existing workflows while built-in `devhost upgrade` covers self-managed upgrades. Must change: stack-run-stop, services-panel, worktree-switch, agent-chat, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: notices-and-feedback, manifest-hot-reload, config-editor (an outcome uses the operation's label in the past tense). Contract: `CC-11`, `CC-31`; SVC-02, SVC-03, WT-02, WT-06, STK-07, CHAT-04.
- **language-08 · One event, one name: accepted and rejected manifest and devhost config changes.** Options: (a) keep "Manifest change not applied" and add what runs and why; every reload names its restarts; an undone rejection says it is resolved; `devhost reload` prints the outcome [manifest-hot-reload]; (b) a save's result accounts for its reload [config-editor]; (c) one change summary per devhost config file save [proxy-settings]; (d) the directory never shows a rejected edit as the stack's configuration [stack-directory]; (e) one grammar for every result [notices-and-feedback]. Decision: all, `WORD-1`, `WORD-4`: applied and not applied on every surface. Reason: Tilt words a Tiltfile load the same in its UI and its log. Must change: manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, proxy-settings, stack-directory, notices-and-feedback. Silent, must adopt: stack-run-stop (the terminal uses the same words), toolbar-shell (the collapsed badge's state for a change not applied, `ACT-7`). Contract: `CC-22`; MAN-03, MAN-04, CFG-04, SET-08.
- **language-09 · Raw internal text shown to people: Rust error chains, paths, JSON, HTML and event tokens.** Options: (a) messages built from a structured error, the location once, **Copy Error** for the original [manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, worktree-switch, stack-run-stop, proxy-settings]; (b) native text only under Details [startup-and-recovery, page-context, agent-chat]; (c) never HTML as an error [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (d) readable event words [running-agents]; (e) exec failures in devhost's words [first-run-setup]; (f) no ANSI escapes in recovery logs [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: all, `FAIL-2`, `WORD-7`, `WORD-8`; agent output keeps the whole error. Reason: Vite's and Next.js's overlays lead with a plain message and collapse the trace. Must change: manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, worktree-switch, stack-run-stop, proxy-settings, startup-and-recovery, page-context, agent-chat, services-panel, notices-and-feedback, running-agents, first-run-setup. Silent, must adopt: serve-folder (file-server errors in words), component-source (source-lookup errors in words; editor launching removed by `O51`), third-party-devtools (library errors in words), log-minimap (colors rendered, never printed as escapes). Contract: none; MAN-04, CFG-03, WT-05, LOG-02.
- **language-10 · Error content: the cause, where to fix it and the next step.** Options: (a) the real cause and its owner, with the failing service's last lines [manifest-hot-reload]; (b) file, line and column with the offending line, close keys, clashes, left-out services and what ran [stack-run-stop]; (c) advice that fits the case [startup-and-recovery, serve-folder]; (d) "Caddy is not installed" with its command, and a log only when this attempt wrote it [first-run-setup]; (e) a deleted worktree labelled deleted [worktree-switch]; (f) an oversized image reported when pasted [agent-chat]. Decision: all, `FAIL-2`, `FAIL-3`, `MENU-5`, `WORD-7`. Reason: rustc and Docker name the cause and its place. Must change: manifest-hot-reload, stack-run-stop, startup-and-recovery, serve-folder, first-run-setup, worktree-switch, agent-chat.
