Silent, must adopt: services-panel (a failed restart names its cause), config-editor (an issue names its key and fix), proxy-settings (a rejected setting names what keeps running). Contract: none; MAN-02, REC-02, SET-02, CHAT-10.
- **language-11 · Outcome sentences: grammar, tense, target and elapsed time.** Options: (a) object first in the toolbar, "api restarted · 2.6 s" [notices-and-feedback, services-panel]; (b) verb first and past tense, a duration after "in", in the terminal [stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, first-run-setup]; (c) an outcome accounts for itself [manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, proxy-settings]; (d) neutral chat lines naming who acted [agent-chat]; (e) a ready line [stack-run-stop, serve-folder]. Decision: (b)'s order on every surface with details after " · ", plus (c), (d), (e); `WORD-4`, `WORD-8`. Reason: one grammar makes the toolbar and the terminal say the same thing, and verb first in the past tense is the reference's default. Must change: notices-and-feedback, services-panel, stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, first-run-setup, manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, proxy-settings, agent-chat, serve-folder. Silent, must adopt: reload-control (the Disable Reloads result), component-source ("Copied path"), page-context (a share's outcome), running-agents (agent events). Contract: `CC-22`; MAN-03, STK-01, CHAT-04.
- **language-12 · A deliberate stop, cancel or denial worded as a failure, and a failure worded as success.** Options: (a) "Stopped by you" without red, no error after Cancel, Denied as a state of its own [agent-chat]; (b) no "waiting for a restart" for devhost's own stops, and a handoff for a replaced stack [stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, manifest-hot-reload]; (c) each end worded by its cause [stack-run-stop, services-panel]; (d) closing the tab picker is a choice [page-context]; (e) Denied in Watch Live Activity [running-agents]. Decision: all, `FAIL-5`, `WORD-7`. Reason: Claude Code marks an interrupt neutrally, and `docker compose stop` reports stops as stopped. Must change: agent-chat, stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, manifest-hot-reload, services-panel, page-context, running-agents. Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery (status pages word devhost's own stops as steps), notices-and-feedback (a stop's outcome uses the neutral style). Contract: `CC-24`; CHAT-04, CHAT-06, STK-07.
- **language-13 · State words across surfaces.** Options: (a) Running, Starting, Restarting, Failed, Exited, Not started, External and Blocked [stack-directory]; (b) running and not started [services-panel]; (c) up, starting, down, Stopping and Disconnected [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (d) starting, ready and exited in `devhost stack info` / `devhost status` (`O62`) [stack-run-stop]; (e) Working, Waiting for you, Idle, Closed [agent-chat]; (f) Working, Waiting for approval since, Idle [running-agents]; (g) not reachable for an external service [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: (a), (b), (e), (g), with (d) and (f) taking the same words, `WORD-6`; (c)'s up and down are retired because down cannot tell failed from exited or blocked (`ACT-2`). Reason: Docker Desktop and Tilt use one set of words in their UI and CLI. Must change: stack-directory, services-panel, notices-and-feedback, stack-run-stop, agent-chat, running-agents, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: toolbar-shell (the badge's accessible name), worktree-switch (switching rows), log-minimap (the service legend). Contract: `CC-25`; TB-03, SVC-01, REC-07, AGT-01.
- **language-14 · Counts and summaries: what each counts and how it is written.** Options: (a) count the whole stack, "3 of 6 running, 3 not started" [services-panel]; (b) a summary naming the services that need the person, up to 3, then +N [services-panel]; (c) no "0 of N" while starting or disconnected [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (d) "5 of 7 running · 1 failed" on cards [stack-directory]; (e) a count with the command that lists [stack-run-stop]; (f) a button carries its count [services-panel]; (g) step counts in progress [worktree-switch]. Decision: all, `WORD-5`, `ACT-6`, `ACT-13`. Reason: Docker Desktop shows Running (3/6), and accessible names spell "3 of 6". Must change: services-panel, notices-and-feedback, stack-directory, stack-run-stop, worktree-switch. Silent, must adopt: running-agents ("2 agents · 1 waiting for you"), toolbar-shell (the collapsed count), agent-chat (messages sent into a running turn and not yet read counted, `STATE-1`), log-minimap (hidden lines counted). Contract: `CC-7`; TB-03, SVC-03, SVC-05, REC-07.
- **language-15 · Empty, loading and waiting states.** Options: (a) an empty page remembers what ended [stack-directory, stack-run-stop]; (b) the running agents control keeps an empty state [running-agents]; (c) a waiting page names the check and the time [startup-and-recovery]; (d) waits name the step and the time [worktree-switch, stack-run-stop]; (e) no Working before anything starts, and a queued follow-up shows at once [agent-chat]; (f) the Redux monitor names its store [third-party-devtools]. Decision: all, `STATE-1`, `STATE-2`, `STATE-3`. Reason: Docker Desktop's and VS Code's empty views name the reason and the action. Must change: stack-directory, stack-run-stop, running-agents, startup-and-recovery, worktree-switch, agent-chat, third-party-devtools. Silent, must adopt: config-editor (loading a file), services-panel (a filter with no match), log-minimap (no output yet), page-context (nothing picked), serve-folder (an empty folder). Contract: `CC-27`, `CC-33`; REC-01, REC-07, AGT-01, CHAT-03, EXT-02.
- **language-16 · Human output and agent output (AGENT=1) as separate contracts.** Options: (a) parseable agent output, `devhost stack info` (alias `devhost status`, `O62`) and a version-matched skill [stack-run-stop]; (b) agent mode gets different instructions [first-run-setup]; (c) chat processes run with `AGENT=1` [agent-chat]; (d) `devhost reload` prints its outcome on one line [manifest-hot-reload]; (e) people get summaries and agents every field [serve-folder, agent-chat]; (f) a client that is not a page gets a short status [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: all, `WORD-9`, `FB-11`, `REV-8`, `SURF-16`, `O62`; `reference/output.md`'s "only the layout differs" changes. Reason: the owner rule that the two outputs differ on purpose; docker and tilt keep machine output beside human tables. Must change: stack-run-stop, first-run-setup, agent-chat, manifest-hot-reload, serve-folder, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (`devhost service` prints both forms), worktree-switch (agents read its terminal lines), proxy-settings (a devhost config file change in both outputs). Contract: `CC-23`, `CC-30`; STK-09, STK-10.
- **language-17 · Capitalization, product names and key names.** Options: (a) Capital Case hints, tools by their display label, the key cap carrying Esc once [page-context]; (b) sentence case for notices, hints and states [agent-chat, page-context, running-agents]; (c) product names, never IDs [agent-chat, running-agents]; (d) Caddy capitalized, with its version [first-run-setup]; (e) one casing for an operation's start and finish lines [worktree-switch]. Decision: title-style action labels and sentence case for everything else, so (b) to (e) and (a)'s key cap; `WORD-3`, `KEY-13`. Reason: the UI package capitalizes action labels, and Apple's guidelines put the rest in sentence case. Must change: page-context, agent-chat, running-agents, first-run-setup, worktree-switch. Silent, must adopt: services-panel, config-editor, notices-and-feedback, stack-run-stop, toolbar-shell, startup-and-recovery (their labels and sentences follow `WORD-3`). Contract: none; CTX-01, CTX-02, SET-02, STK-09.
- **language-18 · Formats for paths, durations, times, sizes, exit codes and signals.** Options: (a) `~` paths shortened in the middle, a service folder only when it differs [worktree-switch, stack-directory]; (b) the file relative to the root manifest with line and column [manifest-hot-reload]; (c) no repeated path, the full path on hover [config-editor]; (d) never break a URL, OSC 8 links, commands on their own line [stack-run-stop]; (e) signals by name with time and sender [stack-run-stop, services-panel]; (f) local time with a relative age [stack-directory]; (g) the SHA-256 of the DER certificate [first-run-setup]. Decision: all, `WORD-8`, with one duration style instead of the five the proposals use. Reason: VS Code shows relative paths with the full path on hover. Must change: worktree-switch, stack-directory, manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, stack-run-stop, services-panel, first-run-setup. Silent, must adopt: agent-chat (sizes in MiB, local times), toolbar-shell (readout units), notices-and-feedback (durations), running-agents (times), log-minimap (timestamps). Contract: none; STK-09, SET-05, WT-01.
- **language-19 · Labels and recorded wording for the owner-settled chat edit modes (O1) and Disable Reloads (O2).** Options: (a) **This worktree (live)** and **Make tickets** (previously with a third **New worktree (review before merge)** mode), with each sent message showing its mode [agent-chat, page-context]; (b) **Disable Reloads** kept, opening the popover [reload-control]; (c) rename it Ask Agent to Disable… [reload-control]; (d) a standing workflow line above the composer [agent-chat]; (e) page data shown in readable rows before Send [page-context]; (f) the agent, model and approval behavior named before Send [agent-chat]. Decision (revision 5, product owner): (a) as two modes only (**This worktree (live)** and **Make tickets**, removing **New worktree (review before merge)** under `O1` q10a6 and adopting worktree under `WORD-11`), (b), (e), (f), `WORD-10`, `WORD-11`, `REV-6`, `SEL-8`; `O2` and `O1` supersede (c) and (d). Reason: owner decisions `O1` (updated 2026-11-18 by q10a6) and `O2` (clarified by `O56`); Cursor shows its mode on every message. Must change: agent-chat, page-context, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: running-agents (shows a chat agent's mode), worktree-switch (names the mode of conversations it leaves behind), notices-and-feedback (the pending Disable Reloads request names the agent). Contract: `CC-10`, `CC-15`; CHAT-08, RLD-01, CTX-05.

### 3.7 status

- **status-01 · One service state model and its names.** Options: (a) every surface shows the published state, and the directory agrees with the toolbar [stack-directory, services-panel]; (b) states named by what happened, Blocked added [stack-directory]; (c) the toolbar's up and down, plus starting, not started and reconnecting [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (d) the CLI shows the same states [stack-run-stop]; (e) the recovery page's distinctions are the reference [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: (a), (d), (e) and (b)'s states, `ACT-2`; one word per state is 2.12's. Reason: only one published state lets surfaces agree; Docker and Tilt keep process state and health apart; (c)'s down cannot tell failed from exited or blocked. Must change: stack-directory, services-panel, notices-and-feedback, stack-run-stop, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (switching services show `ACT-3`'s states), manifest-hot-reload (restarts show as restarting), toolbar-shell (the collapsed badge reads these states, `ACT-7`), serve-folder (a serve session's service too), proxy-settings (moving URLs never changes a state). Contract: `CC-25`; TB-03, SVC-01, REC-02, REC-07, STK-06.
- **status-02 · Starting before readiness.** Options: (a) a starting state with elapsed time and timeout, never red [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (b) the startup page shows health-check progress and only the current attempt [startup-and-recovery]; (c) a polling client gets a small "starting" answer [startup-and-recovery]; (d) the terminal says when the stack is ready, and a detached start shows progress [stack-run-stop]; (e) a starting service keeps its row [services-panel]. Decision: all, `ACT-4`, `ACT-3`, `FB-10`, `SURF-16`, with one look for a first start and a restart and the dependency waited on named. Reason: Docker's "health: starting" and Kubernetes' Running but not Ready. Must change: services-panel, notices-and-feedback, startup-and-recovery, stack-run-stop. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (the target worktree's services show starting, not failed), manifest-hot-reload (restarted services show starting until healthy), stack-directory (starting rows show their elapsed time), serve-folder (a `serve --port` session is starting until its port answers, `FB-5`). Contract: none; REC-01, STK-01, STK-02, SVC-05.
- **status-03 · Stack summaries of a down or partly started stack.** Options: (a) count the whole stack, with not-started services as hollow dots and dimmed rows, reversing the contributor contract [services-panel]; (b) cards show running of total and failures, failed stacks first [stack-directory]; (c) a fixed-width summary at scale [services-panel]; (d) the CLI reports state and exits non-zero while a started service is not running [stack-run-stop]. Decision: all, `ACT-6`, `BAR-1`, `SURF-10`, `FB-11`; a changed service counts as running. Reason: Docker Desktop's "Running (3/6)" and `kubectl`'s READY 2/3; the reversal is `CC-7`. Must change: services-panel, stack-directory, stack-run-stop. Silent, must adopt: toolbar-shell (the collapsed badge summarizes the whole stack, `ACT-7`), notices-and-feedback (outcomes count of the stack's total, `FB-3`), worktree-switch (its outcome counts the stack after the switch), startup-and-recovery (status pages say how many services run). Contract: `CC-7`, `CC-23`; TB-03, SVC-05, REC-07, STK-06.
- **status-04 · A lost connection is not an outage.** Options: (a) the last known dots hollow, "reconnecting…" after about 1.5 s, then Retry Now [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (b) a "Disconnected" chip instead of dots, the cause named, a stopping state and a reconnection line [notices-and-feedback]; (c) writes disabled with a reason [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (d) a port change names the new address [proxy-settings, notices-and-feedback]; (e) stacks say when their proxy is lost and back [first-run-setup, stack-run-stop]. Decision: as `feedback-11`: (a)'s state drawn as unknown without the delay, (b)'s cause, stopping state and reconnection line, (c) to (e); `ACT-5`, `HOLD-2`, `ACT-10`. Reason: Kubernetes marks pods Unknown, not Failed, when it loses their node. Must change: services-panel, notices-and-feedback, proxy-settings, first-run-setup, stack-run-stop. Silent, must adopt: toolbar-shell (the collapsed badge shows the loss first, `ACT-7`), worktree-switch (its own reload is not a loss), manifest-hot-reload (a reload never drops the stream), config-editor (Save unavailable with the reason, `HOLD-2`), stack-directory (a stack it cannot reach shows as unknown, not stopped). Contract: `CC-26`; TB-07.
- **status-05 · Transitional states: restarting, stopping, switching, reloading.** Options: (a) Restart Stack shows per-service progress and a result [services-panel]; (b) a stopping state before services stop, and the terminal says what shutdown waits for [notices-and-feedback, stack-run-stop]; (c) during a switch the old worktree no longer reads running [worktree-switch]; (d) starting one service changes only its row [services-panel, notices-and-feedback]; (e) a reload names its restarts and reports on their rows [manifest-hot-reload]. Decision: all, `ACT-3`, `FB-1`, `FB-3`. Reason: Docker shows Restarting, Kubernetes Terminating, and Tilt keeps the last runtime status beside an update. Must change: services-panel, notices-and-feedback, stack-run-stop, worktree-switch, manifest-hot-reload. Silent, must adopt: stack-directory (shows restarting, stopping and switching as the toolbar does), startup-and-recovery (a restarting service's page says restarting), config-editor (a save's restarts show in the window's restart list, `FB-1`), toolbar-shell (the collapsed badge never shows a transition as failed, `ACT-7`). Contract: none; SVC-02, SVC-04, SVC-05, WT-02, STK-04.
- **status-06 · A failed service inside a stack-wide operation.** Options: (a) one service that cannot start never takes the healthy ones down [services-panel, manifest-hot-reload]; (b) a fixed port another program holds is named and stops nothing [manifest-hot-reload]; (c) `devhost stack restart`, `devhost restart-all` and `devhost upgrade` (`O18`) never leave a slow stack stopped (`O30`) [stack-run-stop]; (d) the outcome reflects the resulting states [manifest-hot-reload, config-editor]; (e) a failed Restart Stack stays where it started [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: all, `FAIL-1`, `UNDO-1`, `REV-11`, `FB-5`, `SURF-3`. Reason: the owner's recovery contract; `error-pages-01`, reproduced by its reviewer and its verifier; Kubernetes keeps old replicas until new ones pass readiness. Must change: services-panel, manifest-hot-reload, stack-run-stop, config-editor, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (a restore that fails too leaves each service in its own state, never all down), notices-and-feedback (the partial outcome counts per `FAIL-1`), stack-directory (shows the failed service and the running rest). Contract: none; SVC-04, MAN-03, STK-07, WT-02.
- **status-07 · Who stopped a service: exit attribution.** Options: (a) devhost's own stops belong to their operation [worktree-switch, stack-run-stop]; (b) a reload's stops are not new exits [manifest-hot-reload]; (c) a kill from outside names its signal, time and sender and keeps that record after a restart [stack-run-stop, services-panel]; (d) a forced stop reads as forced, and a replaced stack says so [stack-run-stop]. Decision: all, `FAIL-5`, `FAIL-3`, `ACT-2`. Reason: systemd separates `code=exited` from `code=killed` and keeps the result after a restart. Must change: worktree-switch, stack-run-stop, manifest-hot-reload, services-panel. Silent, must adopt: startup-and-recovery (the crash view names the signal and the sender), stack-directory (an exited row names its code or signal and time), notices-and-feedback (a deliberate stop never raises a failure notice). Contract: `CC-24`; STK-09, REC-03, SVC-02.
- **status-08 · The cause travels with a down state.** Options: (a) a row says why [services-panel]; (b) output kept per service, frozen at the exit and opened there [startup-and-recovery]; (c) a directory row shows code, time and last line [stack-directory]; (d) a failed check says what it got, a held port names its holder [stack-run-stop, manifest-hot-reload]; (e) a crash-caused rejection shows the line that explains it [manifest-hot-reload]; (f) what devhost ran, and a dropped request in plain words [startup-and-recovery]; (g) a daemon that stops on its own is reported [startup-and-recovery, stack-run-stop]. Decision: all, `ACT-2`, `FAIL-3`, `FAIL-2`, `SURF-13`. Reason: Kubernetes attaches a reason to each state, and the recovery page already knows the cause the stream drops (`apps/devhost/src/services/stack/live.rs:226-291`). Must change: services-panel, startup-and-recovery, stack-directory, stack-run-stop, manifest-hot-reload. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (a blocked service names its worktree's cause), notices-and-feedback (a failure notice carries the cause), toolbar-shell (the trigger's accessible name names failed services, `ACT-13`), config-editor (its banner shows the cause of the exited service it opened for, `SURF-4`). Contract: `CC-25`; SVC-01, REC-02, REC-03, LOG-02.
- **status-09 · What changed means and when it clears.** Options: (a) changed and error are independent, and a restart clears the mark against the new launch [services-panel]; (b) compare with the content at launch, clear on revert, name the files, and say when a hot update already applied the edit [reload-control, services-panel]; (c) Restart Changed with nothing changed names what it restarts [services-panel]. Decision: all, `ACT-8`, `SEL-2`, `SEL-3`. Reason: VS Code compares with the saved content, so undoing an edit clears its mark; Tilt clears a pending update when the new build is live. Must change: services-panel, reload-control. Silent, must adopt: manifest-hot-reload (a reload's restart clears the mark against the new launch), toolbar-shell (the collapsed badge shows changed last, `ACT-7`), stack-directory (changed as a service detail), stack-run-stop (`devhost stack info` / `devhost status` lists changed services, `O62`), worktree-switch (a switch clears marks against the new worktree's launch). Contract: `CC-25`; SVC-03, RLD-02.
- **status-10 · Blocked by an unavailable worktree.** Options: (a) blocked is its own state with the error look and the reason said once per repository [stack-directory]; (b) the recovery page leads with choosing a worktree and never preselects the deleted one [worktree-switch]; (c) the reason is a readable sentence [worktree-switch]; (d) a rejection caused by the deleted worktree clears once the person switches away [manifest-hot-reload]. Decision (revision 2): all, `ACT-2`, `HOLD-4`, `MENU-5`, `SEL-4`, `FAIL-4`, and finding the deleted worktree stops nothing. Reason: a switch never falls back silently (owner rule); Kubernetes keeps a pod Pending with its reason; revision 1 kept the old read side effect that stopped the repository's services (`apps/devhost/src/services/stack/runtime.rs:489-536`; `worktree-switch-14` is now fixed on Rust `main` `190a8bb5`). Must change: stack-directory, worktree-switch, manifest-hot-reload. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (blocked rows show the repository's reason once, never red down), stack-run-stop (the terminal names the blocked repository once instead of printing its URLs), notices-and-feedback (the blocked repository is a condition notice, `ACT-7`), startup-and-recovery (the blocked state's page follows `MENU-5`). Contract: none; WT-05, REC-02, REC-07.
- **status-11 · External services (managed = false).** Options: (a) a page state of its own: not reachable, what devhost routes and checks, the last result, no Restart and no logs [startup-and-recovery, serve-folder]; (b) neutral in the directory, a tag without health [stack-directory]; (c) declaring one never silently turns off switching [manifest-hot-reload, worktree-switch]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): (a) on every surface, the directory included, `ACT-9`; under `O7` (decided 2026-11-25, owner item q17a6), external services never block worktree switches and need no warning when managed services switch (`SEL-5`). Reason: the owner's "status only" limits what devhost does, not what it shows, and one state model (`ACT-2`) needs the directory to agree with the toolbar (`stack-directory-08`); Tilt reports readiness for a resource it does not run; the product owner reversed the external-service switch block on 2026-11-25 so managed services switch freely while external services stay untouched. Must change: startup-and-recovery, serve-folder, stack-directory, manifest-hot-reload, worktree-switch. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (its row shows reachability and the check's time), stack-run-stop (`devhost stack info` / `devhost status` reports reachability, `O62`), notices-and-feedback (an unreachable external service is a condition, never a crash). Contract: none; MAN-07, SVC-01, REC-02.
- **status-12 · Stack-level attention states and the collapsed summary.** Options: (a) collapsing never hides notices, and the badge orders down, rejected, held, changed, healthy [toolbar-shell]; (b) the directory shows a rejected edit as not applied [stack-directory]; (c) a dismissed **Reload blocked** leaves a marker [reload-control]; (d) collapsed chat tells waiting from working [agent-chat]. Decision: all, `ACT-7`, with the lost connection first and an agent waiting after a rejection; `SURF-8`, `UNDO-3`, `UNDO-6`. Reason: the Next.js dev indicator keeps its issue count on the collapsed badge; a routine restart turning the badge red is a defect (`toolbar-shell-10`). Must change: toolbar-shell, stack-directory, reload-control, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: manifest-hot-reload (a rejection raises the badge), notices-and-feedback (each condition is a notice in `FB-8`'s order), services-panel (a restart never raises it), running-agents (a waiting agent raises it). Contract: `CC-20`; TB-02, RLD-02, AGT-01.
- **status-13 · Whole-stack end states.** Options: (a) a stopped stack's hostname names it, when and why it stopped, and the start command [stack-run-stop]; (b) an open page names a stopped stack [notices-and-feedback]; (c) `devhost caddy stop` names the stacks it takes offline, and they say so [first-run-setup, stack-run-stop]; (d) `devhost stop` speaks of the stack, not PIDs [stack-run-stop]; (e) a stop names the conversations it ends [agent-chat, stack-run-stop]; (f) the directory remembers what stopped [stack-directory]; (g) Watch Live Activity says the stack stopped [running-agents]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): all, `ACT-10`, `FB-4`, `SEL-6`, for `O15`'s time, keeping configured HTTPS routes and certificates active for recently stopped stacks while arbitrary unknown HTTPS hostnames keep strict configured-hosts-only TLS (`O63`, decided 2026-11-25). Reason: Docker Desktop keeps a stopped project listed with Start; devhost never opens a browser, so these pages offer the command (owner rule); `O63` keeps stopped-stack recovery working over HTTPS without minting on-demand certificates for arbitrary typos. Must change: stack-run-stop, notices-and-feedback, first-run-setup, agent-chat, stack-directory, running-agents. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (shows the stack stopped, not its services failed), toolbar-shell (the badge shows the stopped stack), startup-and-recovery (a stopped stack's service page says the stack stopped). Contract: `CC-27`; STK-04, STK-08, REC-06, REC-07, AGT-02.
- **status-14 · Reported state matches the processes that run.** Options: (a) a slow stop names what still runs [stack-run-stop]; (b) a killed stack's leftovers are found and named, and a health check passes only for devhost's own process tree [stack-run-stop]; (c) a forced stop reads as forced, and stopping never waits forever [stack-run-stop]; (d) devhost signals only processes it verified as its own [stack-run-stop]; (e) a slow Caddy is not declared stopped [first-run-setup]. Decision (revision 2): all but (b)'s process-tree health check, `ACT-11`, `FAIL-1`, `FAIL-5`: a killed run's recorded leftovers are found, named and refused before launch, and a health check certifies the endpoint. Reason: `interrupted-operations-01` and `process-containment-01` were each reproduced by their reviewer and their verifier against devhost's documented intent; systemd tracks a unit's processes by cgroup. Revision 1 also let a health check pass only for devhost's own process tree, which never passes for the Docker-backed, daemon-lifecycle and wrapper services `apps/devhost/devhost.example.toml` documents. Must change: stack-run-stop, first-run-setup. Silent, must adopt: services-panel, stack-directory, startup-and-recovery and notices-and-feedback (each shows stopped or running only from `ACT-11`'s ground truth, and names the leftovers devhost found). Contract: none; STK-04, STK-06, SET-04.
- **status-15 · State for agents and scripts.** Options: (a) `devhost stack info [--json] [--wait]` (alias `devhost status`, `O62`) with each service's state and an exit code [stack-run-stop]; (b) start and `stack list` print the stack directory's URL [stack-run-stop, stack-directory]; (c) a small status answer for polling clients [startup-and-recovery]; (d) the CLI restarts one service, and every agent error line is marked [stack-run-stop]; (e) a start naming a left-out service names it [stack-run-stop]; (f) chat agents get `AGENT=1` [agent-chat]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): all, `FB-11`, `ACT-2`, `SURF-15`, `SURF-16`, `MENU-6`, `SEL-7`, `O6`, `O62`, `REV-8`; `stack list` keeps its documented picker format, and extended `devhost stack info` (with short alias `devhost status`, `O62`) carries state, changed-file markers and reload-rejection state. Reason: `docker compose ps --format json`, `tilt get` and `kubectl wait`; human and agent output carry the same information (owner rule). Must change: stack-run-stop, stack-directory, startup-and-recovery, agent-chat. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (`devhost stack info` / `devhost status` and the toolbar read one state), notices-and-feedback (agents read outcomes from `devhost stack info` / `devhost status` and the log, `FB-7`), manifest-hot-reload (`devhost reload` and `devhost stack info --wait` / `devhost status --wait` report a reload's outcome). Contract: `CC-11`, `CC-23`; STK-06, STK-09, STK-10.
- **status-16 · Live state and snapshots.** Options: (a) directory statuses update while it is open, and its filter applies as you type [stack-directory]; (b) error pages back off [startup-and-recovery]; (c) only the current attempt's output [startup-and-recovery]; (d) the terminal prints changes, not the stack again [stack-run-stop]; (e) the last known list carries its last-seen time [notices-and-feedback]. Decision: all, `ACT-12`, `ACT-4`, `ACT-5`, `SEL-10`, `FB-10`. Reason: Docker Desktop and Tilt stream state, and one-shot listings carry their age. Must change: stack-directory, startup-and-recovery, stack-run-stop, notices-and-feedback. Silent, must adopt: services-panel and toolbar-shell (show the last-seen time while disconnected), worktree-switch (the worktree list updates while open and says when it was read). Contract: `CC-27`; REC-02, REC-07, STK-09.
- **status-17 · State encoding beyond color, and at scale.** Options: (a) forced colors keep the dots, readout alarms and the hold state apart [toolbar-shell]; (b) health changes and results are announced politely [notices-and-feedback]; (c) each directory status has its own treatment with an icon or text and 4.5:1 contrast [stack-directory]; (d) a fixed-width summary at scale, and a grouped, searchable panel [services-panel]; (e) recovery pages announce the state, not a bare 503 [startup-and-recovery]. Decision: all, `ACT-13`, `FB-9`, `SURF-10`, `SEL-10`, with `SURF-10`'s fit test instead of "about 8". Reason: WCAG 1.4.1 and 1.4.11 and the devtools visual contract. Must change: toolbar-shell, notices-and-feedback, stack-directory, services-panel, startup-and-recovery. Silent, must adopt: worktree-switch (worktree badges have shapes and words), stack-run-stop (terminal states are words, never only colors), running-agents (working and waiting differ in shape and text). Contract: none; TB-03, TB-07, REC-07.
- **status-18 · Which page each state gets, and its lead action.** Options: (a) an app's own 404 and 5xx are the app's responses [startup-and-recovery]; (b) a file server's 404 stays in the file browser [startup-and-recovery, serve-folder]; (c) a crash that needs a manifest fix points at the manifest, and the crash view never covers the failed service's own page [startup-and-recovery]; (d) a blocked service leads with choosing a worktree [worktree-switch]; (e) an external service's page drops Restart and logs [startup-and-recovery, serve-folder]; (f) a hostname with no route names it and suggests a close match [stack-directory]. Decision (revision 6, product owner): all: one page per `ACT-2` state, led by `MENU-5`'s action; `SURF-16`, `SURF-3`, `SEL-10` (see `selection-12`), bounded by `O63` (strict configured-hosts-only TLS for arbitrary unknown HTTPS hostnames, 24 h active HTTPS routes for recently stopped stacks under `O15`, and close-hostname typo suggestions on plain HTTP when `httpEnabled = true` and in the stack directory). Reason: as `selection-12` and `O63`. Must change: startup-and-recovery, serve-folder, worktree-switch, stack-directory. Silent, must adopt: services-panel (a row's lead action matches its state's page, `MENU-5`), notices-and-feedback (a failure notice's lead action follows the same table). Contract: `CC-4`, `CC-27`; REC-02, REC-04, REC-05, REC-06.

### 3.8 Revision 7: items from the reconciliation

The 19 Phase 8 reconciliation units reported gaps in this reference and disagreements between flows; revision 7 settles each one here, merged across the flows that raised it. The entry names those flows, the decision and the rules that carry it. Two items, the folder the install recipe uses (`R7-73`) and whether routing settings in the devhost config file apply live (`R7-74`), are product calls put to the product owner and not decided here.

- **R7-1 · One sign-in sentence (HOLD-7 said "Sign in with your passkey…", O36, WORD-7 and every sibling say "Sign in with passkey…").** Raised by reload-control, log-minimap, running-agents, stack-directory, startup-and-recovery, page-context, config-editor, third-party-devtools. Decision: The majority wording wins: "Sign in with passkey or pair this browser to use devhost here". A surface that gates one thing may end the sentence with what it gates instead of "use devhost here" (O47's "to check stacks from here"; the directory's "to see which …"); nothing else varies. HOLD-7 now quotes it. Where: HOLD-7, WORD-7.
- **R7-2 · Rejection notice: condition (amber) or failure (red).** Raised by manifest-hot-reload, config-editor, notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell. Decision: Condition. A rejected configuration lasts until a configuration is accepted, the stack keeps running the accepted version, and section 1's Terms and `ACT-7` already list it as a condition; red stays for a failed or exited service and a failed operation. The rejection notice, its one-line form and the Configuration window's rejection banner use the condition style (amber) and the ◆ glyph; a crash that caused the rejection keeps its own red crash notice. Where: FB-8, new WF-3.
- **R7-3 · Which surface gives way below 900 px between docked chat and the pinned log panel.** Raised by log-minimap. Decision: Below 900 CSS px wide the two never sit side by side: the one opened earlier folds to its bar control (Chat keeps showing the turn's state, Logs its unread count) and returns as it was, draft, scroll and filter kept, when the later one closes. Folding is not closing: docked chat still closes only by its own **Close** (`KEY-6`). Where: SURF-5, O8 consequence.
- **R7-4 · Restart Stack has no terminal command (MENU-6 exception proposed).** Raised by startup-and-recovery, services-panel. Decision: A command, not an exception: `devhost service restart --all` restarts every managed service inside the running stack, the terminal form of **Restart Stack**, and ships with `CC-11`'s `devhost service` commands. `devhost stack restart` keeps its shipped meaning (replace the supervisor, `O18`), so the label and the command differ by design; the command's help says "Restart Stack in the browser". The crash line and recovery page name it. Exception refused. Where: MENU-6, CC-11.
- **R7-5 · O36 Host clause on the stack directory's hostname.** Raised by running-agents, stack-directory. Decision: The stack directory's hostname (`[caddy].stackDirectoryHost` under the local suffix) counts like a stack's hostname, so this machine's browser there is this machine's. Its relay form is the same label under `[remote].domainSuffix` and always arrives through the connector, so it is never local. devhost links Watch Live Activity only on the directory hostname, on this machine too; `http://127.0.0.1:<port>` keeps answering (`O55`) but nothing links it, and there a replaced stack shows as an ended run with a link to the directory hostname's page, the one remaining exception to `ACT-10`. Where: O36, O55, ACT-10.
- **R7-6 · Focus after a pick and after a menu closes (KEY-5, KEY-6, KEY-10, KEY-12).** Raised by page-context, component-source, reload-control. Decision: A menu returns focus by `KEY-6`: opened with the pointer, to the element that had focus before; opened from the keyboard, to its trigger, which for the source menu in Select Elements is the Select Elements toggle. `KEY-10`'s "(for the source menu, the element it opened on)" is withdrawn. A pointer pick or drawing returns focus to the chat composer, which the pick opened or kept open; a keyboard-latched selection keeps focus on the toggle until Escape. After a reload that no control started (a turn that changed files, `O57`), focus returns to the devhost element that held it before, the composer with its draft, else stays where the browser puts it, because the person was not using devhost. Where: KEY-6, KEY-10, KEY-12, section 4 page-context.
- **R7-7 · Select Again and mark numbers; the gone-mark question.** Raised by page-context, agent-chat. Decision: Numbers are never reused, with one exception: **Select Again** on a number the message cites binds the new pick to that same number. Two fixed questions before Send: "Send with #2 missing? Its element is no longer on this page." and "Send with #1 missing? It is not in page context." (a mark the person removed). Where: SEL-9, WORD-2.
- **R7-8 · Retired settings: warn or refuse.** Raised by component-source, config-editor. Decision: Warn, never refuse. A key devhost retired (such as `[devtools.editor]`, `DEVHOST_EDITOR`) is ignored with a warning naming file, line, column, key and what replaced it, so an upgrade never stops a stack from starting (`O18`); a key devhost never knew stays a rejection. One retired key is still honored: `[devtools.editor].enabled = false` maps onto R9's switch, so no one's off switch flips on upgrade. Where: new FAIL-6.
- **R7-9 · Alt + right-click's off switch once the editor settings are gone.** Raised by component-source. Decision: `[devtools.componentSource].enabled` (manifest, default `true`) turns off the source menu: Alt + right-click and Shift+F10 in Select Elements; page context still resolves source files. `[devtools].enabled = false` removes it with the toolbar. Where: SEL-11, new CC-41.
- **R7-10 · `Copy path` against **Copy Path**.** Raised by component-source. Decision: **Copy Path** (WORD-3 title case for labels); type-ahead still reaches it with c. Where: KEY-10, section 4 component-source.
- **R7-11 · The canonical proxy line.** Raised by proxy-settings, serve-folder, config-editor. Decision: proxy-settings' line is canonical, one line in every command that reports the proxy: "Proxy: HTTPS on <port 443, every interface \| 127.0.0.1:4443 and [::1]:4443>[ and HTTP on …], <reachable from your network \| direct connections from this machine only; relay previews and SSH forwards still reach it> · <config path>", with the macOS warning on the next line when it applies. Browser surfaces say "devbox's network" (the machine name, `O61`) instead of "your network". Where: WORD-9, new WORD-12.
- **R7-12 · The port-moved protocol.** Raised by proxy-settings, notices-and-feedback. Decision: The page shows the move only once the new listener answers: while listeners move it shows the activity line "Moving to port 4443"; when each listener (each bind host, and HTTP when on) confirms, the outcome names the new address with **Open** (path and query kept) and any listener that did not move. One message shape: `listeners-moving` (old and new addresses per listener), then `listeners-moved` or `listeners-failed`. Where: new FB-13.
- **R7-13 · What a signed-out remote page shows.** Raised by proxy-settings, startup-and-recovery. Decision: Only the stack name and the sign-in condition notice with **Sign In with Passkey** and how to pair; no service state, logs, readouts or other notices, because the status and log streams answer only authenticated clients (`O36`). Full pages devhost serves in place of the app (`SURF-16`) still show their status and sentence. Where: HOLD-7.
- **R7-14 · One example hostname set and fixture roster.** Raised by proxy-settings, first-run-setup, stack-directory, third-party-devtools, manifest-hot-reload, reload-control. Decision: Machine `devbox`; stacks Acme Shop (root, services web, api, worker, admin, docs), Acme Admin, Acme Platform; serve sessions `notes`, `demoapp`. Proposals write the product default `.localhost` (`shop.localhost`, `admin.shop.localhost`, `devhost.localhost`); wireframes write the same labels under `.acme.test` (`shop.acme.test`, `devhost.acme.test`); the relay form is the label under `[remote].domainSuffix = "--devbox--preview.acme.test"` in both (`shop--devbox--preview.acme.test`, `devhost--devbox--preview.acme.test`). The stack directory is `devhost.<suffix>` (never `stacks.`). `hotfix-shpo` is the deliberate typo fixture. Where: section 1 Examples, WF-1.
- **R7-15 · IDs for the 2.4 decision rule items ("SURF-4 rule 4" collides with SURF-4).** Raised by log-minimap. Decision: The seven questions become `SD-1` to `SD-7`. Where: 2.4.
- **R7-16 · CC-26's leftover editor clause.** Raised by log-minimap. Decision: Both "becomes" texts drop "editor highlights clear" / React Highlight, removed with editor support (`O51`); the quoted current texts stay as they are. Where: CC-26.
- **R7-17 · Toolbar segment order.** Raised by config-editor, first-run-setup, running-agents, toolbar-shell. Decision: toolbar-shell's order: stack name, Services, Worktrees, CPU, RAM and Disk, Logs, Config, the third-party launchers, Running agents, Hold Reloads, Chat. A segment that is off is absent; the order of the rest never changes. Where: new BAR-5.
- **R7-18 · Copy beside Copy Error when the config editor is off.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload, notices-and-feedback, proxy-settings. Decision: Both: the location with **Copy** (copies `file:line:column`) and **Copy Error** (the whole message), as `KEY-10` already lists. Where: WORD-2.
- **R7-19 · Owner of a dismissed rejection's marker while the config editor is off.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload, notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell. Decision: The stack name, which is always on the bar; Config owns it while the editor is on. Where: SURF-8.
- **R7-20 · When Config shows "not applied".** Raised by config-editor, manifest-hot-reload, notices-and-feedback. Decision: Only after the rejection notice is dismissed (`SURF-8`, `FAIL-4` one report); while the notice shows, it is the report. Where: SURF-8.
- **R7-21 · "Devhost config file" capitalised.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload, proxy-settings, notices-and-feedback, config-editor. Decision: Always "devhost config file" (`WORD-3`: devhost is lowercase, also at a sentence start). Where: WORD-3.
- **R7-22 · No operation label for a hot reload.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload. Decision: **Apply devhost.toml** names the operation (the file's name): activity "Applying devhost.toml · 1 of 2 restarted · 4 s", outcome "Applied devhost.toml: restarted api and worker". Where: WORD-2.
- **R7-23 · Actions for a rejection a crash caused.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload. Decision: `MENU-5` row: **Logs** (the failing service's captured output) leads, then **Edit** (editor on) or the location with **Copy**, then **Copy Error**. Where: MENU-5.
- **R7-24 · A "queued" step.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload. Decision: Already in `FB-1`'s step list; the record's steps (`ACT-1`) name it too. Where: ACT-1.
- **R7-25 · Restart of a service inside a running reload's set.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload. Decision: It queues behind the reload as `CC-8` queues a restart, with **Cancel Restart**; never a second restart at once. Where: REV-12.
- **R7-26 · Loopback addresses in wireframes.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload, proxy-settings, stack-directory. Decision: Allowed where the product's own message names the bind host or a loopback target (`127.0.0.1`, `[::1]`, a port serve session's name); machine hostnames and private domains stay banned. Where: WF-1.
- **R7-27 · Notice-kind classes, state glyphs, legend.** Raised by manifest-hot-reload, startup-and-recovery, proxy-settings, running-agents, stack-directory, page-context. Decision: One glyph set: ● running, ○ not started, ◐ starting or restarting, ✕ exited, ▲ failed, ◆ condition, ? unknown, ⏸ paused; condition notices amber (`banner`), failures red (`banner bad`), successes green, information blue. The legend on every flow page carries them. Where: new WF-2, WF-3.
- **R7-28 · Contract texts with no CC entry.** Raised by startup-and-recovery, proxy-settings, config-editor, reload-control, running-agents, page-context, log-minimap, first-run-setup, stack-directory, third-party-devtools, component-source. Decision: Entries `CC-41` to `CC-51`, one per flow, quote the opening of each changed sentence and point to the proposal's "Backend and contract changes", which quotes both texts in full. Where: 2.14.
- **R7-29 · CC-27 and CC-30 quote docs text Rust main replaced.** Raised by startup-and-recovery, first-run-setup. Decision: Quotes updated to the text on `main` (`managed-caddy.md` "Caddy issues local certificates only…"; `output.md` "Agent mode can include extra diagnostic fields. Exit codes are the same in both modes."). Where: CC-27, CC-30.
- **R7-30 · Check Again routes missing from CC-37.** Raised by startup-and-recovery, stack-directory. Decision: `CC-37` gains both: the service check (`POST /__devhost__/check-service`, a read without an `operationId`) and the directory's stack check (`/stacks.json` and `GET /__devhost__/stacks/ping`). Where: CC-37.
- **R7-31 · WORD-2 labels missing.** Raised by startup-and-recovery, config-editor, reload-control, running-agents, stack-directory. Decision: Added: **Expand** (reopens a minimized crash view), **Retry**, **Show All N Characters**, **Sign In with Passkey**, **Save Anyway**, **Open Conversation** (replaces surfaces-15's Open Chat), **Show Changes**, **Copy**, **Answer**, **Refresh Stacks**, **Apply devhost.toml**. **Reload Now** is the one label for applying a held or blocked reload; bare **Reload** is retired. Where: WORD-2, UNDO-3.
- **R7-32 · SURF-16 with an empty or non-HTML answer.** Raised by startup-and-recovery. Decision: devhost wraps it in its own document showing the status and the body verbatim. Where: SURF-16.
- **R7-33 · O15's 24 h window on relay preview hostnames.** Raised by startup-and-recovery. Decision: Confirmed: the window covers every hostname the stack had, relay forms included. Where: O15.
- **R7-34 · Trust line against heading-first.** Raised by startup-and-recovery, first-run-setup. Decision: Full pages put the heading first, then condition banners such as the trust line. One trust sentence, first-run-setup's: "devbox does not trust devhost's local certificate authority, so browsers warn on each devhost hostname and block a page's requests to another one. On devbox, run devhost caddy trust in a terminal, then reload; on another Mac, run devhost caddy trust-remote with devbox's SSH name there." Where: FB-9, WORD-7.
- **R7-35 · REV-8 under AGENT=1 for every sudo step.** Raised by proxy-settings. Decision: Every step that asks for a password (`caddy trust`, `privileged-ports`) refuses under `AGENT=1` and names the command a person runs. Where: REV-8.
- **R7-36 · WORD-9 download size of unknown length.** Raised by first-run-setup, proxy-settings. Decision: Exception accepted: when the server sends no length, the size shows as it arrives and in the outcome. Where: WORD-9.
- **R7-37 · Listener confirmation per bind host and for HTTP.** Raised by proxy-settings. Decision: Part of R12. Where: FB-13.
- **R7-38 · Network-reach wording on browser surfaces.** Raised by proxy-settings. Decision: "devbox's network" (R11). Where: WORD-12.
- **R7-39 · Window-frame sequence tables.** Raised by proxy-settings. Decision: A `.win` frame that is not a screen says "Diagram, not a screen" in its title. Where: WF-4.
- **R7-40 · Exited-service banner in the Configuration window lacks Restart.** Raised by config-editor. Decision: `SURF-4`'s banner leads with **Restart** per `MENU-5`. Where: SURF-4.
- **R7-41 · Logs in the Configuration window.** Raised by config-editor. Decision: **Show All Output** expands in the window (a modal holds focus); section 4's config-editor line no longer sends it to the pinned panel. Where: section 4.
- **R7-42 · Save while a save applies.** Raised by config-editor. Decision: It waits behind the running one and says so (`REV-12`); section 4's "Save is disabled" is withdrawn. Where: REV-12, section 4.
- **R7-43 · Editing a manifest of a worktree the stack does not run.** Raised by config-editor, worktree-switch. Decision: Allowed; the save is checked, kept and applies when the stack runs that worktree; the outcome says so. Where: UNDO-6.
- **R7-44 · devhost's modal over the page's modal dialog; source menu inside it.** Raised by config-editor, component-source, toolbar-shell. Decision: devhost's modal surfaces and menus open above the page's dialog in the top layer; the source menu joins the notices inside the dialog. Where: SURF-11.
- **R7-45 · 2.11 rows for the configuration text and the log panel.** Raised by config-editor, log-minimap. Decision: Rows added: Enter and Mod+Z in the configuration text; the separator's arrows, Home and End; Escape in the panel; Escape on the peek. Where: 2.11.
- **R7-46 · Outcome of Restore Running Version; in-window problem entries.** Raised by config-editor. Decision: "Restored devhost.toml to the version Acme Shop runs"; problem entries follow `WORD-7` with file, line and column first. Where: WORD-4, WORD-7.
- **R7-47 · Row word for a service a save touches.** Raised by config-editor. Decision: No new word: its state (restarting) with the cause "devhost.toml saved" (`ACT-2`). Where: WORD-6.
- **R7-48 · Reload Page while chat is closed.** Raised by reload-control, agent-chat. Decision: The turn's outcome notice carries "Reload to see the change" with **Reload Page**. Where: FB-12.
- **R7-49 · Where the reloads-disabled mark lives.** Raised by reload-control. Decision: On the server with the request record, never browser storage, so a relay phone cannot reload past it. Where: O56, new CC-45.
- **R7-50 · Strings the popover shares with the composer.** Raised by reload-control. Decision: agent-chat owns the approval line, the second-agent question and `HOLD-5` reasons; reload-control quotes them. Where: REV-5.
- **R7-51 · CC-15's project-target clause.** Raised by reload-control. Decision: Dropped: the request targets the page's worktree (`O1`). Where: CC-15.
- **R7-52 · HOLD-1 exception for the Disable Reloads popover.** Raised by reload-control. Decision: Accepted: the popover shows its `HOLD-5` reason in place of Send. Where: HOLD-1.
- **R7-53 · Section 4 reload-control entry omits three decisions.** Raised by reload-control. Decision: Adds selection-14, review-17, surfaces-01. Where: section 4.
- **R7-54 · A Disable Reloads result waiting on a restart.** Raised by reload-control. Decision: Group (3) of `FB-8`, neutral, with its actions. Where: FB-8.
- **R7-55 · Second-agent question while the agent scan is incomplete.** Raised by running-agents, agent-chat. Decision: Accepted: it also asks while the scan is incomplete, naming the agents devhost could not check. Where: REV-3, CC-14.
- **R7-56 · Tab-title prefix.** Raised by running-agents, agent-chat. Decision: `ACT-7`'s "(1) waiting · " is canonical; Watch Live Activity's title is "Watch Live Activity · Acme Shop". Where: O16, ACT-7.
- **R7-57 · WORD-6 state for an agent whose CLI records none.** Raised by running-agents. Decision: "state not reported" added. Where: WORD-6.
- **R7-58 · WORD-10 labels.** Raised by running-agents. Decision: Prompt and Stopped added. Where: WORD-10.
- **R7-59 · ACT-10 stop-reason sentences and close codes.** Raised by running-agents, stack-directory. Decision: One sentence per reason ("Stopped from the terminal at 14:02", "Stopped with Ctrl-C at 14:02", "Stopped after 30 min idle", "Replaced by devhost restart-all at 14:02", "Chat turned off at 14:02"); a stream closes 1001 when its stack ends and 1000 when a setting turned the feature off. Where: ACT-10.
- **R7-60 · Stack-name keys in URL paths; reserved names.** Raised by stack-directory. Decision: Key: the stack-name slug, plus six hex of the root manifest path only on a clash; `self` and `ping` are reserved and always take the suffix. Where: SEL-6.
- **R7-61 · Unknown as a stack state.** Raised by stack-directory. Decision: A stack whose devhost process does not answer reads "unknown" (`WORD-6`, `ACT-6`). Where: WORD-6.
- **R7-62 · Directory heading with only stopped stacks.** Raised by stack-directory. Decision: "No stacks running · 2 stopped in the last 24 hours". Where: STATE-2.
- **R7-63 · Labels for a stack's manifest list and the attention group.** Raised by stack-directory. Decision: "Manifest files"; "Needs you". Where: WORD-1.
- **R7-64 · Page-private JSON endpoints; side-effecting GETs.** Raised by stack-directory. Decision: No GET changes state; page-private routes live under `/__devhost__/` and are documented as internal. Where: REV-12.
- **R7-65 · MENU-5 on read-only surfaces.** Raised by stack-directory. Decision: Accepted: the lead action is a link to the page that performs it plus its terminal command with **Copy**; only **Check Again** runs in place. Where: MENU-5.
- **R7-66 · Terminal label of the directory.** Raised by first-run-setup, stack-directory. Decision: `O61` already decides it: a terminal line that points to a page uses that page's words, "All stacks on devbox: https://devhost.localhost/". Where: O61.
- **R7-67 · Untrusted certificate authority as a condition.** Raised by first-run-setup, notices-and-feedback, toolbar-shell. Decision: Accepted into `ACT-7` after a rejected configuration; its dismissed marker sits on the stack name. Where: ACT-7.
- **R7-68 · trust-remote's SSH target.** Raised by first-run-setup. Decision: The command's argument, never `machineName`, which only labels. Where: O61.
- **R7-69 · Certificate dates and fingerprints.** Raised by first-run-setup. Decision: Dates as 2027-11-25; SHA-256 as colon-separated uppercase pairs, shown whole. Where: WORD-8.
- **R7-70 · Exit codes for a declined question and a cancel.** Raised by first-run-setup. Decision: Declined: 1 ("Not done: you answered no"); Ctrl-C: 130. Where: FAIL-5.
- **R7-71 · Section 4 first-run-setup lines.** Raised by first-run-setup. Decision: The caddy stop question and "devhost start downloads Caddy" are removed (`O52`). Where: section 4.
- **R7-72 · CC-13 gaps.** Raised by first-run-setup. Decision: `CC-13` gains `print-root-cert --fingerprint`, the trusted-roots record and the Install page's Update and Uninstall sections (`CC-48`). Where: CC-13, CC-48.
- **R7-73 · Install recipe folder.** Raised by first-run-setup. Decision: Owner question q85, asked; not decided here. Where: owner.
- **R7-74 · config.toml routing settings live.** Raised by proxy-settings. Decision: Owner question q84, asked; not decided here. Where: owner.
- **R7-75 · HOLD-1 for a library's own conditions.** Raised by third-party-devtools. Decision: Not a 2.6 block: a library that is absent, an unsupported version or the person's choice is the launcher's state, shown as a short state beside its name with the sentence in its tooltip, accessible description and Devtools row. Where: HOLD-1.
- **R7-76 · TanStack's held source-inspector key.** Raised by third-party-devtools. Decision: Removed from 2.11: devhost launches TanStack Devtools without its source inspector, which opens an editor (`O51`). Where: 2.11.
- **R7-77 · Dock beside right- or top-edge library panels.** Raised by third-party-devtools, toolbar-shell. Decision: A right-edge panel shifts the dock and docked chat inward by its width (as the minimap does); a top-edge panel moves a top-corner dock below it. Where: SURF-1.
- **R7-78 · A page reload clearing a launcher failure.** Raised by third-party-devtools. Decision: A launcher failure is a page action, not an operation: a reload clears it, and its log line keeps it (named exception to `FB-6`). Where: FB-6.
- **R7-79 · Redux and TanStack labels on toolbar-shell.** Raised by third-party-devtools, toolbar-shell. Decision: Library product names (`WORD-3`). Where: WORD-3.
- **R7-80 · Banner for a reference gap that is not a cross-flow conflict.** Raised by third-party-devtools. Decision: Two banner kinds: "Open conflict" (flows disagree) and "Proposed exception" (a flow departs from a rule); both go once the reference settles them. Where: WF-5.
- **R7-81 · Duration on a reload outcome when nothing restarted.** Raised by third-party-devtools, toolbar-shell. Decision: No duration: "Reloaded devtools settings; nothing restarted". Where: WORD-4.
- **R7-82 · KEY-1 text entry and ARIA widgets.** Raised by toolbar-shell. Decision: Accepted: roles textbox, searchbox, combobox and spinbutton count as text entry; grid does not. Where: KEY-1.
- **R7-83 · Chat turn progress while chat is closed.** Raised by agent-chat. Decision: Accepted into `FB-1`: on the Chat button (working, waiting, reply ready), not as an activity line. Where: FB-1.
- **R7-84 · Alt-hold start and end.** Raised by page-context. Decision: `KEY-2` gains: it ends when Alt is released or another key is pressed. Where: KEY-2.
- **R7-85 · Crop preview modal.** Raised by page-context. Decision: Accepted as `SURF-3`'s one other modal: person-opened, Escape cancels. Where: SURF-3, SD-3.
- **R7-86 · Where an inline outcome shows.** Raised by page-context. Decision: `FB-2` gains: a panel's status line, a popover in place of its buttons, docked chat as the turn summary. Where: FB-2.
- **R7-87 · MENU-1 log panel status line.** Raised by log-minimap. Decision: Accepted: an entry point to the service it names. Where: MENU-1.
- **R7-88 · Design-language gaps (gutter marks, tooltips, comboboxes, inert surfaces, phone frame, tab strip, colored terminal output, callout order, step limits).** Raised by config-editor, page-context, running-agents, stack-directory, first-run-setup, reload-control, third-party-devtools. Decision: Conventions recorded as `WF-1` to `WF-6`; the shared classes are Stage C. Where: 2.15.

## 4. Per-flow conformance

Each flow lists what its proposal (P) and its wireframe (W) must specify, written before the proposals (each flow's proposal and wireframe now apply them and record where they differ as a proposed exception or a conflict), and what today's app already does that the redesign keeps (Conforms). Rule IDs point to section 2, entry IDs to section 3 and O IDs to section 5; every section 3 entry that names a flow under Must change or Silent, must adopt is cited in that flow's lines. Items common to every flow are not repeated below:

- Acceptance criteria cite rule IDs, and findings by finding ID.
- Keys use Mod notation, come only from the 2.11 table, and draw their hints from one definition (`KEY-13`, `BAR-4`); focus moves and returns per `KEY-6`, never to the page body; Escape follows `KEY-8` and Enter `KEY-9`.
- Wherever the flow writes, it shows the write-blocking states of 2.6: the lost connection (`HOLD-2`, `ACT-5`), another operation in progress (`HOLD-3`), an unavailable or blocked worktree (`HOLD-4`), chat that cannot take a message (`HOLD-5`), a rejected configuration, which blocks nothing (`HOLD-6`), and a control a browser cannot use where it is, the unauthenticated state on a page opened from another environment (`HOLD-7`, `O36`); an unavailable action stays in place with its reason (`MENU-3`, `HOLD-1`).
- Service and stack states come only from the published state, one word per state on every surface and in both outputs (`ACT-2`, `ACT-6`, `WORD-6`), told by shape and text (`ACT-13`), with a lost connection drawn as unknown, never down (`ACT-5`).
- Each operation reports once: progress in one place (`FB-1`), one outcome per page where the progress was (`FB-2`), successes that close by themselves and failures that stay (`FB-6`), one announcer, and a busy control that keeps focus (`FB-9`, `KEY-11`).
- Visible words follow `WORD-1` to `WORD-11` (glossary, worktree for every working copy, one title-style label per operation, progress and outcome grammar, counts, state words, failures, formats); terminal lines follow both output contracts (`WORD-9`, `FB-10`, `FB-11`).
- Empty, loading, unknown and cannot-load states follow `STATE-1` to `STATE-4`.
- Surfaces follow 2.4's decision rule; a question appears in place, never in a modal dialog, an outcome notice or `confirm()` (`SURF-2`, `REV-4`); devhost takes page input only where it draws (`SURF-11`, `KEY-5`), and its notices and announcer keep working under the page's native and scripted modal dialogs (`SURF-11`, `FB-9`).
- Acceptance viewports: 1600x1000, 1280x800 and 900x900 CSS pixels, in the light and the dark theme, and 1280x800 at 200% zoom (640x400) (`SURF-9`); narrower windows and the 320 CSS pixel phone layouts for chat, approvals and notices follow `O8`.
- Accessibility: WCAG 2.2 AA in both themes and in forced colors: the devtools contrast contract (4.5:1 text, 3:1 control boundaries), full pages reflowing at 320 CSS pixels, no focused page element entirely hidden by a devhost surface (a fixture page with three notices, docked chat compact and the log panel open, tabbed through), pointer targets of 24 CSS pixels or 2.5.8's spacing, and the toolbar under a scripted modal dialog (a Radix Dialog fixture) (`SURF-9`, `SURF-11`, `FB-9`).

Section 4 is also the reconciliation ledger: after a reconciliation run lands, its integration step ends each flow's entry with one "Reconciled in run" line, and the open issues that line lists are the flow's next work.

### agent-chat

- P: Before Send the composer names the conversation or that Send starts one, the agent and model chip, the edit mode (**This worktree (live)** or **Make tickets**, `O1` q10a6) with the worktree it edits, what devhost adds and a line when the mode changes (`O20`), another worktree's conversation answerable and stoppable but taking no message, with **New Conversation** offered (`CC-38`), whether the agent asks before acting, and the page context item by item (`SEL-8`, `REV-6`, `O1`, `O3`, `O13`, `O20`; `selection-08`, `review-09`, `language-19`); the conversation list holds every worktree's conversations by title, agent, model, worktree label and state, filtered once it overflows (`SEL-4`, `SEL-10`; `selection-04`, `selection-13`); settings persist per stack and page origin (`SEL-11`, `CC-9`; `selection-14`); page context tells the agent the page's URL, service and worktree (`SEL-3`; `selection-03`).
- P: The two modes behave as section 5 states, a manifest's `prompt` sent first and each mode's own instructions after it, deciding where the agent edits (`O35`): **This worktree (live)** edits the active worktree in place (main's included, no additional worktree, commit or merge; `O1`, `O19`, `CC-10`), and when a turn changed watched code of a service that does not reload by itself, the turn's summary says so ("api runs the old code until it restarts") and offers **Restart Changed** so the person restarts it (`O58`, reversed 2026-11-18); **Make tickets** reports filed tickets or changes no files and says it cannot make tickets when it lacks that capability, with no drafts (`O11`, `O21`, reversed 2026-11-18); each turn lists the files it changed (`O22`) and ends per `FB-12` (once reloads are disabled, `"Reload to see the change"` with **Reload Page** and no auto-reload, `O56`; with Hold Reloads on, devhost applies held reloads once unless focus is in a text field, `O57`), and the Disable Reloads request shows in its conversation with its mode (`REV-5`, `O27`; `review-08`); a browser in another environment authenticates with one shared devhost passkey under a single owned HTTPS domain (working seamlessly on phones via synced passkeys or hybrid/QR sign-in) or falls back to a one-time `devhost pair` link, while an unauthenticated remote client sees the unauthenticated state (`HOLD-7`, `O36`, decided 2026-11-25, `O59`).

### config-editor

### proxy-settings

- P: A devhost config file change names the stacks whose URLs move before it is saved and in its outcome, is checked before Caddy moves, and applies without a question (`REV-10`, `REV-11`, `SEL-6`, `SEL-11`; `selection-06`, `review-03`, `review-07`, `language-01`); devhost adds neither a `publicUrl` reference nor `DEVHOST_PUBLIC_URL` (`O45`, reversed 2026-11-18); a rejected change names `config.toml` and the line, leads to it, says the running settings keep running, and its fix reports them restored (`MENU-4`, `FAIL-2`, `FAIL-4`, `HOLD-6`; `selection-10`, `feedback-12`, `language-10`); there is no Undo save, and the way back is to what runs (`UNDO-6`; `review-06`); stale saves are refused (`REV-13`; `review-14`); steps that download or ask for a password say so first, and trust changes only by a person (`REV-8`, `WORD-9`; `review-05`); configured-hosts-only TLS stays strict for arbitrary unknown HTTPS hostnames while recently stopped stacks keep HTTPS routes active for `O15`'s 24 h window (`O63`).
- P: The outcome certifies only once the new listener answers, reports per stack whose URLs moved, in each affected stack's terminal and on pages whose URL it moved, with the new address and **Open** (`FB-3`, `FB-5`, `FB-10`, `HOLD-2`; `feedback-02`, `feedback-03`, `feedback-04`, `feedback-05`, `feedback-11`, `feedback-15`); a rejection is a condition that clears when a configuration is accepted, and the Configuration window shows it in its banner (`ACT-7`, `SURF-4`; `feedback-09`, `surfaces-04`); moving URLs never changes a service's state, and pages that lose their stream to the move show unknown with the cause (`ACT-2`, `ACT-5`; `status-01`, `status-04`); both outputs report the change (`WORD-9`, `FB-11`; `language-16`).
- W: Draw the devhost config file in the Configuration window with Save's statement of moved stacks, a rejection banner, and a page whose URL moved showing the new address with **Open** (path and query kept).
- W: Words: devhost config file and shared Caddy settings, never settings file; stacks by name, never IDs; one event name for accepted and rejected changes; outcome grammar; no raw Rust error chains (`WORD-1`, `WORD-4`, `WORD-7`; `language-02`, `language-06`, `language-08`, `language-09`, `language-11`); reach lines name the machine ("Saving makes 3 stacks reachable only from devbox: …", `O61`).
- Conforms: Caddy listening on every interface by default is documented (owner rule); on macOS, `bindHost = "127.0.0.1"` listens on loopback from port 1024 upward, and below 1024 (including default 443) keeps listening on all interfaces while printing an explicit warning on every start naming `httpsPort = 4443` or a privileged launcher (`O39`, decided 2026-11-25, `proxy-settings-01`); who may use the stacks' controls from another environment is `O36`'s check (decided 2026-11-25: shared-domain passkey with phone support + `devhost pair` fallback); a rejected file keeps the running shared settings.

- P: Serve sessions appear in `devhost restart-all`'s and `devhost upgrade`'s printed sets by their 2.12 name and keep running under them (`SEL-6`, `REV-7`, `O18`, `O31`, `CC-36`; `selection-06`, `review-04`); `devhost serve`'s trust step follows `REV-8` (`review-05`), and stopping a serve session uses `O30`'s unified shutdown timeout; a `serve` service's manifest error names file and line (`MENU-4`; `selection-10`); devhost's file server answers a missing file with its own 404 inside the file browser, never the recovery page, and inapplicable actions are absent (`SURF-16`, `MENU-5`, `MENU-3`, `O5`; `selection-11`, `selection-12`, `surfaces-03`, `status-18`); Markdown's **Show source** says whether it applies to one file or every Markdown page (`SEL-11`; `selection-14`); the toolbar keeps its placement per served folder (`SURF-7`, `CC-5`; `surfaces-07`); `devhost serve` keeps chat on by default, stated on its first line, with `--no-chat` to turn it off, while `--port` never hands an agent the shell's current folder (`O40`, reversed 2026-11-18), and remote access follows `O36`'s check (decided 2026-11-25: shared-domain passkey with phone support and `devhost pair` fallback).

- Conforms: Markdown views render YAML front matter as a table; a serve session ends with Ctrl-C in its terminal; serve sessions keep chat on by default with `--no-chat` (`O40`, decided 2026-11-18).

- P: `devhost caddy stop` names the running stacks and the hostnames it takes offline, then acts without asking, because it ends no agent's turn, and takes `--dry-run` (`SEL-6`, `REV-1`, `REV-7`, `CC-12`; `selection-06`, `review-03`, `review-04`, `surfaces-13`); `devhost caddy trust`, `trust-remote` and `untrust` refuse under `AGENT=1` before any `sudo`, `trust-remote` shows the fingerprint and asks, every install prints its removal command, and help names steps that download or ask for a password (`REV-8`, `UNDO-5`, `WORD-9`, `CC-13`; `review-05`); a devhost config file error at `caddy start` names file and line (`MENU-4`; `selection-10`); `caddy start` and `caddy stop` keep one name in help, docs and output (`MENU-6`; `selection-15`).

- W: Draw the terminal transcripts, human and `AGENT=1`, for a first start after Caddy's own steps, which come first (`O52`; revision 7), reaching the trust step, a trust refusal under `AGENT=1`, `trust-remote` with its fingerprint, and `caddy stop` with its set, asking nothing (revision 7).
- W: Words: help and output say stack; outcome grammar; title-style command names in docs; formats for sizes, fingerprints and paths; both outputs (`WORD-1`, `WORD-3`, `WORD-4`, `WORD-8`, `WORD-9`; `language-01`, `language-11`, `language-16`, `language-17`, `language-18`); the certificate line names the machine whose trust it reports ("devbox does not trust devhost's local certificate authority, …") and, for a browser elsewhere, the remedy `devhost caddy trust-remote` (`O61`).
- Conforms: devhost never opens a browser; the trust step runs only when a person can answer, and an untrusted CA is a documented path; one managed Caddy per state directory routes every stack.

### notices-and-feedback

- P: This flow owns the notice stack, the activity line, outcome and condition notices, the announcer and the lost connection (Work no flow owns): progress in one place, else one activity line; one outcome where the progress was, read from the operation record and surviving the page reload it caused; successes close after `O14`'s time, with the countdown paused only for a notice that arrives while the browser window is not focused (`document.hasFocus()` false) until focus returns, while failures stay until handled (`FB-1`, `FB-2`, `FB-6`, `ACT-1`, `O14`, `O53`; `feedback-01`, `feedback-02`, `feedback-03`); each outcome uses the name of the control that started it, names its targets, the conversation a send went to, and counts of the stack's total (`FB-3`, `MENU-6`, `SEL-8`; `selection-08`, `selection-15`, `status-03`); a starting service never draws running ones as down (`SEL-7`, `ACT-4`; `selection-07`, `status-02`); Restart Changed's outcome names its set (`SEL-1`, `SEL-2`; `selection-01`); a key whose action is unavailable reports why (`MENU-3`; `selection-11`); stale writes are refused with the current state (`REV-13`; `review-14`); no question ever sits in an outcome notice (`REV-4`, `SURF-2`; `review-17`).

### Work no flow owns

The reference owns these until a flow's proposal claims them; each names the flow expected to claim it.

- The shared announcer, its two live regions and its move into the topmost modal (`FB-9`), the notice stack's order and "N more" (`FB-8`), and the operation record every surface reads (`ACT-1`): owned here until notices-and-feedback, the feedback foundation, claims them; every flow's notices use them.
- The one binding definition behind handlers, hints and the docs' Keyboard section (`KEY-13`, `BAR-4`, `CC-29`), and the dock landmark (`KEY-3`): owned here until toolbar-shell claims them.
- The shared worktree list and the shared recovery view, rendered by one component in both style entries (`SURF-14`, `SEL-4`, `MENU-5`): owned here until worktree-switch (the list) and startup-and-recovery (the view) claim them; stack-directory, services-panel and the status pages reuse them.
- The agent output commands `devhost stack info [--json] [--wait]` (short alias `devhost status`, `O62`), `devhost reload` and `devhost logs` (`FB-11`, `CC-23`) and the `devhost service` commands (`SEL-7`, `CC-11`): owned here until stack-run-stop claims them, with services-panel for the service commands' behavior.
- The glossary terms added to `CONTEXT.md` and the docs' label and worktree-wording changes (`WORD-1`, `WORD-11`, `CC-31`, `CC-32`): owned here; each flow lands the labels it uses with its own change.
- The layout route and record the control server keeps per manifest or served folder (`SURF-7`, `CC-5`) and the per-stack chat settings (`SEL-11`, `CC-9`): owned here until toolbar-shell and agent-chat claim them.

## 5. Product decisions

Product calls that the evidence could not settle. Each entry states the question and its status (**Decided**, **Withdrawn**, **Retired**, or **Recommended default** while awaiting the owner), with the evidence for and against; section 5.1 records the product owner's review across all three review pages on 2026-11-18 and the four rules added from the owner's comments (`HOLD-8`, `ACT-14`, `FB-12` in revision 4, and `WORD-11` in revision 5), and section 5.2 records the final Phase 7 review on 2026-11-25 (`q17`, `q40`, `q46`, `q49`, `q79`, `q80`, `q81`). Of the 63 entries: **60 are Decided** (`O1`–`O49`, `O52`–`O59`, `O61`–`O63`: `O1`, `O2` and `O44` prior or updated on 2026-11-18; 39 approved as recommended on 2026-11-18: `O3`–`O6`, `O8`–`O11`, `O13`–`O15`, `O17`, `O19`, `O20`, `O22`–`O29`, `O31`–`O35`, `O37`, `O38`, `O42`, `O43`, `O46`–`O49`, `O52`, `O54`, `O55`, `O57`; 9 reversed or decided by comment on 2026-11-18: `O12`, `O16`, `O21`, `O30` [parallel-shutdown design settled 2026-11-25], `O40`, `O45`, `O53`, `O56`, `O58`; 3 settled by default on 2026-11-18: `O41`, `O59`, `O61`; and 6 decided or reversed on 2026-11-25: `O7`, `O18`, `O36`, `O39`, `O62`, `O63`), **2 are Withdrawn** (`O50`, `O51`, no editor support), **1 is Retired** (`O60`, by `O51`), and **0 remain Awaiting owner** (with zero open follow-ups remaining on `O18` or `O30`). O14, O15, O28, O30, O34, O41, O42, O46, O48, O49 and O59 are calibrated: Phase 7 measured each number, or modelled it where nothing could be measured, and each records the evidence with both margins, the nearest alternative and why it fails, the pitfalls and how it ships (`O30` pairs its superseded sequential calibration with the settled parallel-shutdown budget that fits 30 s). Every rule that implements a decision cites its entry ID: `SEL-8` and `REV-6` (O3), `SEL-2` (O4), `SURF-16` and `MENU-5` (O5), `SEL-7` (O6), `SEL-5`, `ACT-9` and `HOLD-4` (O7), `SURF-9` (O8), `SEL-11` and `FB-3` (O9), `MENU-5` and `MENU-6` (O10), `UNDO-5` (O11, O19, O21, O22), `REV-9` (O12), `REV-6` (O13, O20), `FB-6` (O14, O53), `ACT-10` and `STATE-2` (O15, O63), `ACT-7` (O16), `KEY-2` (O17), `WORD-2` and `MENU-6` (O18), `UNDO-4` (O23), `FB-5` (O24, O28), `REV-5` (O25, O26, O27), `UNDO-6` (O29), `ACT-11` (O30), `FAIL-5` (O31), `MENU-5` (O32), `REV-7` (O33), `KEY-2` (O34), `REV-6` (O35), `HOLD-5` and `HOLD-7` (O36, O38, O54), `HOLD-8` (O36, O39), `WORD-7` (O54), `SURF-15` (O55), `FB-12` (O56, O57, O58), `MENU-1`, `MENU-5`, `WORD-1`, `WORD-2` and `STATE-2` (O61), `FB-11` and `WORD-2` (O62), `SEL-10` (O63), and `WORD-11` (from `O7`'s 2026-11-18 comment).

The research notes of five flows (agent-chat, reload-control, config-editor, worktree-switch and stack-run-stop) asked 35 open questions. Sixteen are O19 to O33, each citing its note and question (agent-chat question 5 and reload-control question 7 share O22). The other nineteen were already answered where they belong: agent-chat question 1 by O1, `UNDO-5` and `CC-10`, and question 7 by section 1 and `O36` (who can reach chat, which `HOLD-8` brings into this reference); reload-control question 4 by `FB-5` (devhost restarts nothing by itself; the result offers the restart) and question 6 by `UNDO-3` and `SURF-8` (turning the hold off keeps the held reload pending with **Reload Now**, the note's own default; a dismissed held reload leaves its marker); config-editor question 1 by `UNDO-6`, question 2 by `REV-12` and `FB-5`, question 3 by `UNDO-2` (`CC-19`), question 4 by `HOLD-7` and `O38`, question 5 by `REV-10` and question 8 by `UNDO-3`; stack-run-stop question 4 by `FB-4` and `REV-7`, question 5 by `SEL-6` (`CC-34`) and question 6 by `KEY-14`; worktree-switch questions 1 and 2 by `SEL-5`, question 3 by `REV-1` and `REV-3`, question 4 by `REV-1` and `ACT-10`, question 5 by `HOLD-4` and question 7 by `FB-10`.

O36 to O51 came from the flow designs (Phase 6): each is a call that a flow's proposal left to the product owner as an open conflict, recorded with the default that flow assumed (O44 departs from it and says why) and the flows that apply it. O36 to O40, O47, O50, O54 and O55 bear on remote use. The product owner made using devhost from another environment a primary use case (`HOLD-8`, from the owner's comment of 2026-11-18 on owner item a2: no protection may confine a feature to this machine; remote execution is made safe for that use; `bindHost` on macOS is fixed) and answered a2 with a2a2: no loopback-only stopgap ships, the agent-chat redesign builds `O36`'s check, `O39`'s fix ships with it, and owner item q7 is moot. Revision 3's defaults for these entries assumed a loopback-only check; each reworked entry keeps that reasoning in one sentence. O8 and O10 changed their evidence with the rule, and O8 its default. Section 1 no longer leaves network reach outside this reference: `O36` decides it (shared-domain passkey with phone support and `devhost pair` fallback, decided 2026-11-25), and `HOLD-7` describes the unauthenticated state. O41, O42, O46, O48 and O49 are calibrated thresholds (all decided or settled on 2026-11-18). O44 asks how a route's `path` matches and is decided: prefix routing (owner item q9); its former second call, the starter manifest, is O52 (decided).

O52 and O53 came from Phase 7 (owner items q62 and q63), O54 and O55 from `HOLD-8` (q64 and q65), O56 to O58 from `FB-12` (q66 to q68), O59 and O60 from the network rework (q69 and q70), O61 from `HOLD-8` (q77; from q77 on, an owner ID is no longer its entry's O number plus ten, since q71 to q76 are owner items on other work), and O62 and O63 from the Rust `main` cutover (`190a8bb5`) audit in Phase 7 round 2 (q80 and q81, both decided on 2026-11-25 alongside `O7`, `O18`, `O36` and `O39`, plus `O30`'s parallel-shutdown settlement). On 2026-11-18 the owner decided O52, reversed O53, approved O54 and O55, reversed O56, approved O57, reversed O58, settled O59 and O61 by default, withdrew O50 and O51 ("no editor support"), and retired O60.

- **O1 How a coding agent handles a chat message: the chat edit mode. Decided (product owner, 2026-11-16; updated 2026-11-18 by owner item q10a6): the chat composer offers an explicit, visible choice of two modes only — This worktree (live) and Make tickets — with no additional worktree mode.** The product owner's comment of 2026-11-18 on owner item q10 (answer q10a6), verbatim: “lets simplify this, changes are always made to whichever worktree user has active (main including), no additional worktrees”. **This worktree (live)** edits whichever worktree the person has active (main's included), leaves every change uncommitted, and creates no additional worktree, commit or merge; it is the default when the message carries page context (and for a new conversation without page context, `O3`). **Make tickets** has the agent file tickets for the problem instead of editing, relying on the agent's own skills (`O11`), or changes no files and says it cannot make tickets when it lacks that capability (`O21`). Every sent message shows which mode applied; nothing commits, merges or creates worktrees.
